  "description": "## 概要\n\nSpotify の再生履歴（`me/player/recently-played`）を Cloudflare Containers 上で PyIceberg を使って R2 Data Catalog に Apache Iceberg 形式で保存するパイプライン。Phase 1 は `recently_played` のみ、後続で `top_tracks` / `saved_tracks` / `audio_features` を追加する拡張可能な設計。\n\ndbt（`modeling/`）が後段の変換を担う前提の **ELT 指向**。Iceberg レイヤは raw 近似（完全ネスト構造をそのまま保持）、dbt の stg/marts で flatten と集計を行う。\n\n---\n\n## 決定事項サマリ\n\n| # | 項目 | 決定 |\n|---|---|---|\n| Q1 | 対象データ | **Phase 1: `me/player/recently-played` のみ**（将来 top/saved/audio-features を追加） |\n| Q2 | アーキテクチャ | **PyIceberg on Cloudflare Containers**（R2 Data Catalog REST 経由） |\n| Q3 | 初回 OAuth | 手元のローカルスクリプトで 1 回だけ認可 → refresh_token を取得 |\n| Q4 | 可変状態の置き場 | **Workers KV** に refresh_token と `last_played_at` カーソル |\n| Q5 | 実行頻度 | **毎時**（`0 * * * *`） |\n| Q6 | スキーマ/パーティション | **完全ネスト + `_raw_json` 補助列 + `days(played_at)` hidden partition** |\n| Q7 | 責務分担 | **Fat Container**（Python 集中）+ `outboundByHost` で Worker 側 bindings を露出 |\n\n---\n\n## アーキテクチャ全体像\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Cloudflare Worker (spotify-iceberg)                              │\n│                                                                  │\n│   cron: 0 * * * *  ──►  scheduled handler                        │\n│                            │                                     │\n│                            ▼                                     │\n│         env.CONTAINER_DO.idFromName(\"spotify\")                   │\n│              → stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\")                │\n│                                                                  │\n│   outboundByHost:                                                │\n│     \"kv.internal\"  → env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.{get,put}              │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                             │ internal HTTP\n                             ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Cloudflare Container (Durable Object backed)                     │\n│ image: python:3.12-slim + spotipy + pyiceberg[pyarrow]           │\n│                                                                  │\n│   FastAPI server on :8080                                        │\n│     POST /run  →  pipeline.run_recently_played()                 │\n│                                                                  │\n│   pipeline steps:                                                │\n│     1. GET http://kv.internal/refresh_token    (via outbound)    │\n│     2. Spotify /api/token → access_token                         │\n│        (if refresh_token rotated) PUT http://kv.internal/...     │\n│     3. GET http://kv.internal/cursor                             │\n│     4. GET spotify.com/v1/me/player/recently-played?after=...    │\n│     5. pyarrow.Table 整形（完全ネスト + _raw_json + _ingested_at）│\n│     6. catalog.load_table((\"spotify\",\"recently_played\")).append  │\n│     7. PUT http://kv.internal/cursor = max(played_at)            │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                             │ HTTPS\n                             ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg REST)                                   │\n│   warehouse: data-lake  / namespace: spotify                     │\n│   table:     recently_played                                     │\n│                                                                  │\n│ R2 Bucket: data-lake                                             │\n│   data/spotify/recently_played/<day>/*.parquet                   │\n│   metadata/spotify/recently_played/*.json                        │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 設計のポイント\n\n- **Container = Durable Object**。cron の毎時起動でコンテナを起動し、`sleepAfter` を短めに設定してスピンダウンさせることで課金時間を最小化。\n- **Worker は薄い 2 役のみ**: ①cron を受けて Container に投げる、②`outboundByHost` で KV を露出。\n- **PyIceberg の扱うこと**: Parquet ファイルを R2 S3 API 経由でアップロード、manifest と snapshot を生成、REST Catalog に commit — 全て 1 つの `table.append(arrow_table)` 呼び出しで完結。\n- **R2 Data Catalog の namespace**: `spotify`、table: `recently_played`。将来 `spotify.top_tracks_daily`, `spotify.saved_tracks` を同じ namespace に増やす。\n- **Worker 側の secrets**: なし（KV は binding で解決）。\n- **Container 側の secrets**: `SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID`, `SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET`, `R2_CATALOG_URI`, `R2_CATALOG_TOKEN`, `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `R2_ENDPOINT`, `R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE`。\n\n---\n\n## コンポーネント詳細\n\n### Worker (`spotify-iceberg/worker/`)\n\n**責務**: cron トリガ、Container DO への fetch、outbound binding ハンドラ\n\n**ファイル構成**:\n```\nspotify-iceberg/\n├── worker/\n│   ├── src/\n│   │   ├── index.ts          # fetch/scheduled handler + Container DO export\n│   │   └── outbound.ts       # outboundByHost handlers (kv.internal)\n│   ├── wrangler.jsonc\n│   ├── package.json\n│   └── tsconfig.json\n```\n\n**wrangler.jsonc（抜粋）**:\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"name\": \"spotify-iceberg\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-04-20\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"triggers\": { \"crons\": [\"0 * * * *\"] },\n  \"kv_namespaces\": [\n    { \"binding\": \"SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\", \"id\": \"<created-via-wrangler>\" }\n  ],\n  \"containers\": [\n    {\n      \"class_name\": \"SpotifyContainer\",\n      \"image\": \"./container/Dockerfile\",\n      \"max_instances\": 1,\n      \"instance_type\": \"basic\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"durable_objects\": {\n    \"bindings\": [\n      { \"class_name\": \"SpotifyContainer\", \"name\": \"SPOTIFY_CONTAINER\" }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"migrations\": [\n    { \"new_sqlite_classes\": [\"SpotifyContainer\"], \"tag\": \"v1\" }\n  ],\n  \"observability\": { \"enabled\": true, \"head_sampling_rate\": 1 }\n}\n```\n\n**`src/index.ts` のスケルトン**:\n```typescript\nimport { Container } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\nexport class SpotifyContainer extends Container {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\"; // TODO: 実測して調整\n}\n\nSpotifyContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"kv.internal\": async (request, env: Env) => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1);\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const value = await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get(key);\n      return new Response(value ?? \"\", { status: value ? 200 : 404 });\n    }\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put(key, await request.text());\n      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n    }\n    return new Response(\"Method not allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nexport default {\n  async scheduled(_event, env, ctx) {\n    const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n    const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n    const res = await stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    console.log(`container /run → ${res.status}`);\n  },\n} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;\n```\n\n### Container (`spotify-iceberg/container/`)\n\n**責務**: Spotify API 呼び出し、Iceberg 書き込み\n\n**ファイル構成**:\n```\nspotify-iceberg/container/\n├── Dockerfile\n├── pyproject.toml             # uv で管理\n├── uv.lock\n└── src/\n    ├── main.py                # FastAPI app\n    ├── pipeline.py            # run_recently_played() オーケストレーション\n    ├── spotify.py             # Spotify API クライアント（spotipy ラップ）\n    ├── schema.py              # PyIceberg Schema 定義\n    ├── state.py               # KV 読み書き（outbound 経由）\n    └── iceberg_writer.py      # pyarrow → pyiceberg append\n```\n\n**主要依存**:\n```toml\n[project]\ndependencies = [\n  \"fastapi>=0.110\",\n  \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.27\",\n  \"spotipy>=2.24\",\n  \"pyiceberg[pyarrow,s3fs]>=0.7\",\n  \"pyarrow>=17\",\n  \"httpx>=0.27\",\n]\n```\n\n**pipeline 疑似コード**:\n```python\nasync def run_recently_played():\n    refresh_token = await state.get(\"refresh_token\")\n    access_token, new_refresh = spotify.refresh(refresh_token)\n    if new_refresh != refresh_token:\n        await state.put(\"refresh_token\", new_refresh)\n\n    cursor = await state.get(\"cursor\") or \"0\"\n    items = spotify.recently_played(access_token, after_ms=int(cursor))\n    if not items:\n        return {\"rows\": 0}\n\n    table = iceberg_writer.build_arrow_table(items)\n    iceberg_writer.append(\"spotify.recently_played\", table)\n\n    latest = max(int(i[\"played_at_ms\"]) for i in items)\n    await state.put(\"cursor\", str(latest))\n    return {\"rows\": len(items), \"new_cursor\": latest}\n```\n\n**Iceberg Catalog 接続**:\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.catalog.rest import RestCatalog\n\ncatalog = RestCatalog(\n  \"r2\",\n  uri=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_URI\"],\n  token=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\"],\n  warehouse=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\"],\n  **{\n    \"s3.endpoint\": os.environ[\"R2_ENDPOINT\"],\n    \"s3.access-key-id\": os.environ[\"R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\"],\n    \"s3.secret-access-key\": os.environ[\"R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\"],\n    \"s3.region\": \"auto\",\n  },\n)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Iceberg スキーマ詳細\n\n### テーブル識別子\n\n- **Catalog**: `r2` (RestCatalog)\n- **Namespace**: `spotify` *(TODO: 後で再検討)*\n- **Table**: `recently_played`\n- **Full identifier**: `r2.spotify.recently_played`\n\n### PyIceberg Schema 定義\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.schema import Schema\nfrom pyiceberg.types import (\n    NestedField, StructType, ListType,\n    StringType, IntegerType, LongType, BooleanType, TimestamptzType,\n)\n\nARTIST_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(101, \"id\",   StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(102, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(103, \"uri\",  StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(201, \"id\",           StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(202, \"name\",         StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(203, \"uri\",          StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(204, \"album_type\",   StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(205, \"release_date\", StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(206, \"total_tracks\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n)\n\nTRACK_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(301, \"id\",          StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(302, \"name\",        StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(303, \"uri\",         StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(304, \"duration_ms\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(305, \"explicit\",    BooleanType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(306, \"popularity\",  IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(307, \"isrc\",        StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(308, \"album\",       ALBUM_STRUCT,  required=False),\n    NestedField(\n        field_id=309, name=\"artists\",\n        field_type=ListType(\n            element_id=310,\n            element_type=ARTIST_STRUCT,\n            element_required=True,\n        ),\n        required=True,\n    ),\n)\n\nCONTEXT_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(401, \"type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(402, \"uri\",  StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nRECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA = Schema(\n    NestedField(1, \"played_at\",    TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(2, \"played_at_ms\", LongType(),        required=True),\n    NestedField(3, \"track\",        TRACK_STRUCT,      required=True),\n    NestedField(4, \"context\",      CONTEXT_STRUCT,    required=False),\n    NestedField(5, \"_raw_json\",    StringType(),      required=True),\n    NestedField(6, \"_ingested_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    identifier_field_ids=[1, 301],\n)\n```\n\n### 設計判断のメモ\n\n- **`played_at_ms: long` を冗長に持つ理由**: Spotify の `after=` パラメータは epoch ms を要求する。dbt 側や BI 側から直接取り出しやすくするため保持。\n- **`album.release_date: string` の理由**: Spotify 仕様で `YYYY` / `YYYY-MM` / `YYYY-MM-DD` が混在するため、`DateType` では型不一致で落ちる。dbt 側で `release_date_precision` と組み合わせて正規化。\n- **`artists: list<struct>` を `required=True`**: Spotify 仕様上必ず 1 件以上ある。\n- **`_raw_json: required=True`**: 保険列として必須化。将来スキーマに無い新フィールドも dbt で拾える。\n- **`identifier_field_ids`**: 論理 PK の宣言用。PyIceberg の append では強制されないが、後で MERGE INTO で活用できる。\n\n### Partition Spec\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.partitioning import PartitionSpec, PartitionField\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import DayTransform\n\nPARTITION_SPEC = PartitionSpec(\n    PartitionField(\n        source_id=1, field_id=1000,\n        transform=DayTransform(), name=\"played_at_day\",\n    ),\n)\n```\n\n### Sort Order\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.table.sorting import SortOrder, SortField\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import IdentityTransform\n\nSORT_ORDER = SortOrder(\n    SortField(source_id=1, transform=IdentityTransform()),\n)\n```\n\n### テーブル初期化フロー\n\n```python\ndef ensure_table() -> Table:\n    if not catalog.table_exists((\"spotify\", \"recently_played\")):\n        catalog.create_namespace_if_not_exists(\"spotify\")\n        catalog.create_table(\n            identifier=(\"spotify\", \"recently_played\"),\n            schema=RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA,\n            partition_spec=PARTITION_SPEC,\n            sort_order=SORT_ORDER,\n            properties={\n                \"write.format.default\": \"parquet\",\n                \"write.parquet.compression-codec\": \"zstd\",\n                \"format-version\": \"2\",\n            },\n        )\n    return catalog.load_table((\"spotify\", \"recently_played\"))\n```\n\n初回実行時に自動作成される。手動 bootstrap 不要。\n\n---\n\n## データフロー・差分管理・エラーハンドリング\n\n### 差分管理カーソル（正常系）\n\nSpotify `recently-played` の `after` パラメータ仕様: epoch ms、strict `>`、新しい順に最大 50 件。\n\nKV に `cursor = <最後に取り込んだ played_at_ms>` を保存 → 次回は `after=<cursor>` で厳密に差分。\n\n### 初回ブートストラップ\n\n**方針**: `cursor` 未設定なら `after=<1時間前>` を送る。\n\n```python\nFIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000  # 1 hour\n\ncursor = await state.get(\"cursor\")\nif cursor is None:\n    after_ms = int(time.time() * 1000) - FIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_MS\nelse:\n    after_ms = int(cursor)\n```\n\n過去履歴が欲しい場合は Spotify Streaming History JSON を別途申請・手動投入（Phase 1 スコープ外、TODO）。\n\n### エラー分類と対処\n\n**Transient（自動リトライ）**:\n\n| 種別 | 判定 | 対処 |\n|---|---|---|\n| Spotify 5xx | HTTP 500–599 | exponential backoff 3 回（0.5s, 1s, 2s） |\n| Spotify 429 | HTTP 429 | `Retry-After` ヘッダに従う（上限 30s） |\n| R2 Catalog/S3 5xx | HTTP 5xx | pyiceberg 内の httpx/boto3 のリトライに任せる |\n| ネットワーク瞬断 | `ConnectionError` | exponential backoff 3 回 |\n\n**Permanent（ログ + 停止、cursor 据え置き）**:\n\n| 種別 | 判定 | 対処 |\n|---|---|---|\n| refresh_token 失効 | Spotify `invalid_grant` | CRITICAL ログ + 例外 raise、再認可が必要 |\n| Spotify scope 不足 | 403 + `insufficient_scope` | 同上 |\n| R2 Catalog 認証失敗 | 401/403 | CRITICAL ログ + 例外 raise |\n| Iceberg schema 不整合 | pyarrow cast 失敗 | 1 件ずつ処理、失敗分は `_dlq/` へ JSON 退避 |\n\n### ハーフ成功（冪等性）\n\n**危険なケース**: Iceberg commit 成功 → KV cursor 更新失敗。次回 cron で重複取得。\n\n**Phase 1 方針**: ベストエフォート、**重複は dbt 側で dedup 吸収**。`stg_spotify__recently_played` で `QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY played_at, track.id ORDER BY _ingested_at DESC) = 1`。\n\n### シーケンス図\n\n```\nWorker.scheduled          Container /run         KV(outbound)        Spotify           R2/Iceberg\n     │                         │                      │                 │                  │\n     │─── cron fire ──────────>│                      │                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET /refresh_tok ─>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<──── 200 OK ─────────│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── POST /api/token ──────────────────── │                  │\n     │                         │<─── access_token ────────────────────  │                  │\n     │                         │   (refresh_token rotated?)             │                  │\n     │                         │── PUT /refresh_tok ─>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 204 ─────────────│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET /cursor ──────>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 200 \"17135...\" ──│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET recently-played?after=... ────── │                  │\n     │                         │<─── items[] ─────────────────────────  │                  │\n     │                         │  build pyarrow.Table                   │                  │\n     │                         │── table.append(arrow) ─────────────────────────────────── │\n     │                         │<───────────────── snapshot_id ──────────────────────────── │\n     │                         │── PUT /cursor ──────>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 204 ─────────────│                 │                  │\n     │<─── 200 {rows: N} ──────│                      │                 │                  │\n```\n\n### 観測性\n\n- **Worker**: `console.log` で container 呼び出し結果（Workers Observability で自動収集）\n- **Container**: Python `logging` を JSON 形式で stdout へ\n- **主要ログフィールド**: `run_id` (UUID), `rows_written`, `new_cursor`, `snapshot_id`, `spotify_api_latency_ms`, `iceberg_commit_latency_ms`\n- **アラート**: `invalid_grant` / 認証失敗は `log level=ERROR` + `alert=true` フィールド（通知は TODO）\n\n---\n\n## 開発・テスト・デプロイ\n\n### セットアップ手順（初回 1 回のみ）\n\n```\n1. R2 Data Catalog 有効化\n     wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n\n2. Spotify Developer アプリ作成\n     https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard\n     Redirect URI: http://localhost:8888/callback\n\n3. refresh_token を手元で取得\n     uv run python scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py\n\n4. KV namespace 作成\n     wrangler kv namespace create SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\n\n5. 初期値を KV に投入\n     wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV \\\n       \"refresh_token\" \"<手順3の値>\"\n\n6. Container 側 secrets 登録（7本）\n     wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID\n     wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_URI\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\n     wrangler secret put R2_ENDPOINT\n     wrangler secret put R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\n     wrangler secret put R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\n\n7. デプロイ\n     cd spotify-iceberg && pnpm wrangler deploy\n\n8. 初回トリガ（手動確認）\n     wrangler tail で cron 発火を観察\n```\n\n**必要な Spotify scope**: `user-read-recently-played`\n\n### `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py`\n\n```python\nimport spotipy\nfrom spotipy.oauth2 import SpotifyOAuth\n\nauth = SpotifyOAuth(\n    client_id=input(\"Client ID: \"),\n    client_secret=input(\"Client Secret: \"),\n    redirect_uri=\"http://localhost:8888/callback\",\n    scope=\"user-read-recently-played\",\n    open_browser=True,\n    cache_path=None,\n)\ntoken_info = auth.get_access_token(as_dict=True)\nprint(f\"refresh_token = {token_info['refresh_token']}\")\n```\n\n### テスト戦略\n\n**Python ユニット** (`container/tests/`):\n\n| ファイル | 対象 | 手法 |\n|---|---|---|\n| `test_schema.py` | `schema.py` | fixture の pyarrow Table で cast 確認 |\n| `test_spotify.py` | `spotify.py` | `responses` で Spotify API モック、refresh rotate / 0件 / 50件 / 429 網羅 |\n| `test_iceberg_writer.py` | `iceberg_writer.py` | SQLite-backed `SqlCatalog` + local fs で本物の R2 に触らず検証 |\n| `test_pipeline.py` | `pipeline.py` | 全統合、httpx で outbound KV もモック |\n\n**Worker ユニット** (`worker/src/__tests__/`):\n- `outbound.test.ts`: `kv.internal` が GET/PUT に正応答、未知ホストは 404\n\n**E2E 手動**:\n1. `wrangler tail` で cron 発火ログを観察\n2. DuckDB から読み取り:\n   ```sql\n   INSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;\n   ATTACH '<R2_CATALOG_URI>' AS r2 (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT_TYPE S3_TABLES);\n   SELECT played_at, track.name, track.artists[1].name\n   FROM r2.spotify.recently_played\n   ORDER BY played_at DESC LIMIT 10;\n   ```\n\n**CI（GitHub Actions）**:\n- `pytest container/`、`pnpm -C worker test:run`\n- Lint: `biome check worker/`, `ruff check container/`\n\n### デプロイ運用\n\n| 場面 | 操作 |\n|---|---|\n| コード変更 | PR → CI pass → main merge → Workers Builds が自動デプロイ |\n| secret rotation | `wrangler secret put` |\n| KV 手動修正 | `wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV cursor <new_ms>` |\n| schema 追加 | コード変更 → deploy、Iceberg が自動進化（append 時） |\n| ロールバック | Worker は dashboard から前リビジョンへ |\n\n---\n\n## 実装フェーズ分割\n\n| Phase | 内容 | 成果物 |\n|---|---|---|\n| **1.1** | プロジェクトスキャフォールド（worker/, container/, Dockerfile, wrangler.jsonc） | deploy 可能な空の Worker + Container |\n| **1.2** | 手元 refresh token 取得スクリプト + KV セットアップ検証 | `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py` で取得可能 |\n| **1.3** | Container に Spotify 呼び出し実装（Iceberg 切り離しスタブ） | `/run` POST で items 件数を返す |\n| **1.4** | PyIceberg 書き込み + テーブル自動作成 | 実データが Iceberg に書かれ DuckDB で読める |\n| **1.5** | 差分管理（cursor）+ Worker cron 配線 + outbound KV ハンドラ | 毎時 cron で実データが積み上がる |\n| **1.6** | エラーハンドリング + ログ整備 + テスト | CI green、本番運用開始 |\n\nPhase 2 以降（別 issue）: `top_tracks_daily`, `saved_tracks`, `audio_features` enrichment, Streaming History bulk backfill, Workflows 化。\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions（TODO）\n\n- [ ] Container の `sleepAfter` — 初期値 5m、実運用で Worker 起動時間を見て調整\n- [ ] 将来 Cloudflare Workflows に巻き取るか — retry/observability と複雑さの天秤\n- [ ] Iceberg namespace 名 \"spotify\" 妥当性 — 他プロジェクトとの整合性\n- [ ] 過去履歴バックフィル — Spotify Streaming History JSON からの一括 append スクリプト\n- [ ] Iceberg commit 失敗時の dead-letter — items を R2 に JSON 退避\n- [ ] アラート通知 — `invalid_grant` / 認証失敗時の Slack or メール通知\n- [ ] secret を Cloudflare Secrets Store に移行するか\n\n---\n\n## 成功基準\n\n- 毎時の cron で Iceberg テーブルに新しい再生履歴が追記される\n- DuckDB から `SELECT * FROM r2.spotify.recently_played` でクエリできる\n- dbt（`modeling/`）から stg モデルとして flatten 可能な構造になっている\n- 1 週間エラーなしで稼働する\n- Container の月間コストが $1 未満に収まる（毎時起動 × 数秒実行）\n"
}

{"id":"TA-469","title":"Spotify → R2 Iceberg データパイプライン設計","description":"## 概要\n\nSpotify の再生履歴（`me/player/recently-played`）を Cloudflare Containers 上で PyIceberg を使って R2 Data Catalog に Apache Iceberg 形式で保存するパイプライン。Phase 1 は `recently_played` のみ、後続で `top_tracks` / `saved_tracks` / `audio_features` を追加する拡張可能な設計。\n\ndbt（`modeling/`）が後段の変換を担う前提の **ELT 指向**。Iceberg レイヤは raw 近似（完全ネスト構造をそのまま保持）、dbt の stg/marts で flatten と集計を行う。\n\n---\n\n## 決定事項サマリ\n\n| \\# | 項目 | 決定 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| Q1 | 対象データ | **Phase 1:** `me/player/recently-played` のみ（将来 top/saved/audio-features を追加） |\n| Q2 | アーキテクチャ | **PyIceberg on Cloudflare Containers**（R2 Data Catalog REST 経由） |\n| Q3 | 初回 OAuth | 手元のローカルスクリプトで 1 回だけ認可 → refresh_token を取得 |\n| Q4 | 可変状態の置き場 | **Workers KV** に refresh_token と `last_played_at` カーソル |\n| Q5 | 実行頻度 | **毎時**（`0 * * * *`） |\n| Q6 | スキーマ/パーティション | **完全ネスト +** `_raw_json` 補助列 + `days(played_at)` hidden partition |\n| Q7 | 責務分担 | **Fat Container**（Python 集中）+ `outboundByHost` で Worker 側 bindings を露出 |\n\n---\n\n## アーキテクチャ全体像\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Cloudflare Worker (spotify-iceberg)                              │\n│                                                                  │\n│   cron: 0 * * * *  ──►  scheduled handler                        │\n│                            │                                     │\n│                            ▼                                     │\n│         env.CONTAINER_DO.idFromName(\"spotify\")                   │\n│              → stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\")                │\n│                                                                  │\n│   outboundByHost:                                                │\n│     \"kv.internal\"  → env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.{get,put}              │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                             │ internal HTTP\n                             ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ Cloudflare Container (Durable Object backed)                     │\n│ image: python:3.12-slim + spotipy + pyiceberg[pyarrow]           │\n│                                                                  │\n│   FastAPI server on :8080                                        │\n│     POST /run  →  pipeline.run_recently_played()                 │\n│                                                                  │\n│   pipeline steps:                                                │\n│     1. GET http://kv.internal/refresh_token    (via outbound)    │\n│     2. Spotify /api/token → access_token                         │\n│        (if refresh_token rotated) PUT http://kv.internal/...     │\n│     3. GET http://kv.internal/cursor                             │\n│     4. GET spotify.com/v1/me/player/recently-played?after=...    │\n│     5. pyarrow.Table 整形（完全ネスト + _raw_json + _ingested_at）│\n│     6. catalog.load_table((\"spotify\",\"recently_played\")).append  │\n│     7. PUT http://kv.internal/cursor = max(played_at)            │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n                             │ HTTPS\n                             ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│ R2 Data Catalog (Iceberg REST)                                   │\n│   warehouse: data-lake  / namespace: spotify                     │\n│   table:     recently_played                                     │\n│                                                                  │\n│ R2 Bucket: data-lake                                             │\n│   data/spotify/recently_played/<day>/*.parquet                   │\n│   metadata/spotify/recently_played/*.json                        │\n└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 設計のポイント\n\n* **Container = Durable Object**。cron の毎時起動でコンテナを起動し、`sleepAfter` を短めに設定してスピンダウンさせることで課金時間を最小化。\n* **Worker は薄い 2 役のみ**: ①cron を受けて Container に投げる、②`outboundByHost` で KV を露出。\n* **PyIceberg の扱うこと**: Parquet ファイルを R2 S3 API 経由でアップロード、manifest と snapshot を生成、REST Catalog に commit — 全て 1 つの `table.append(arrow_table)` 呼び出しで完結。\n* **R2 Data Catalog の namespace**: `spotify`、table: `recently_played`。将来 `spotify.top_tracks_daily`, `spotify.saved_tracks` を同じ namespace に増やす。\n* **Worker 側の secrets**: なし（KV は binding で解決）。\n* **Container 側の secrets**: `SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID`, `SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET`, `R2_CATALOG_URI`, `R2_CATALOG_TOKEN`, `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`, `R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`, `R2_ENDPOINT`, `R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE`。\n\n---\n\n## コンポーネント詳細\n\n### Worker (`spotify-iceberg/worker/`)\n\n**責務**: cron トリガ、Container DO への fetch、outbound binding ハンドラ\n\n**ファイル構成**:\n\n```\nspotify-iceberg/\n├── worker/\n│   ├── src/\n│   │   ├── index.ts          # fetch/scheduled handler + Container DO export\n│   │   └── outbound.ts       # outboundByHost handlers (kv.internal)\n│   ├── wrangler.jsonc\n│   ├── package.json\n│   └── tsconfig.json\n```\n\n**wrangler.jsonc（抜粋）**:\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"name\": \"spotify-iceberg\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-04-20\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"triggers\": { \"crons\": [\"0 * * * *\"] },\n  \"kv_namespaces\": [\n    { \"binding\": \"SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\", \"id\": \"<created-via-wrangler>\" }\n  ],\n  \"containers\": [\n    {\n      \"class_name\": \"SpotifyContainer\",\n      \"image\": \"./container/Dockerfile\",\n      \"max_instances\": 1,\n      \"instance_type\": \"basic\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"durable_objects\": {\n    \"bindings\": [\n      { \"class_name\": \"SpotifyContainer\", \"name\": \"SPOTIFY_CONTAINER\" }\n    ]\n  },\n  \"migrations\": [\n    { \"new_sqlite_classes\": [\"SpotifyContainer\"], \"tag\": \"v1\" }\n  ],\n  \"observability\": { \"enabled\": true, \"head_sampling_rate\": 1 }\n}\n```\n\n`src/index.ts` のスケルトン:\n\n```typescript\nimport { Container } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\nexport class SpotifyContainer extends Container {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\"; // TODO: 実測して調整\n}\n\nSpotifyContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"kv.internal\": async (request, env: Env) => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1);\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const value = await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get(key);\n      return new Response(value ?? \"\", { status: value ? 200 : 404 });\n    }\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put(key, await request.text());\n      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n    }\n    return new Response(\"Method not allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nexport default {\n  async scheduled(_event, env, ctx) {\n    const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n    const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n    const res = await stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    console.log(`container /run → ${res.status}`);\n  },\n} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;\n```\n\n### Container (`spotify-iceberg/container/`)\n\n**責務**: Spotify API 呼び出し、Iceberg 書き込み\n\n**ファイル構成**:\n\n```\nspotify-iceberg/container/\n├── Dockerfile\n├── pyproject.toml             # uv で管理\n├── uv.lock\n└── src/\n    ├── main.py                # FastAPI app\n    ├── pipeline.py            # run_recently_played() オーケストレーション\n    ├── spotify.py             # Spotify API クライアント（spotipy ラップ）\n    ├── schema.py              # PyIceberg Schema 定義\n    ├── state.py               # KV 読み書き（outbound 経由）\n    └── iceberg_writer.py      # pyarrow → pyiceberg append\n```\n\n**主要依存**:\n\n```toml\n[project]\ndependencies = [\n  \"fastapi>=0.110\",\n  \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.27\",\n  \"spotipy>=2.24\",\n  \"pyiceberg[pyarrow,s3fs]>=0.7\",\n  \"pyarrow>=17\",\n  \"httpx>=0.27\",\n]\n```\n\n**pipeline 疑似コード**:\n\n```python\nasync def run_recently_played():\n    refresh_token = await state.get(\"refresh_token\")\n    access_token, new_refresh = spotify.refresh(refresh_token)\n    if new_refresh != refresh_token:\n        await state.put(\"refresh_token\", new_refresh)\n\n    cursor = await state.get(\"cursor\") or \"0\"\n    items = spotify.recently_played(access_token, after_ms=int(cursor))\n    if not items:\n        return {\"rows\": 0}\n\n    table = iceberg_writer.build_arrow_table(items)\n    iceberg_writer.append(\"spotify.recently_played\", table)\n\n    latest = max(int(i[\"played_at_ms\"]) for i in items)\n    await state.put(\"cursor\", str(latest))\n    return {\"rows\": len(items), \"new_cursor\": latest}\n```\n\n**Iceberg Catalog 接続**:\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.catalog.rest import RestCatalog\n\ncatalog = RestCatalog(\n  \"r2\",\n  uri=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_URI\"],\n  token=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\"],\n  warehouse=os.environ[\"R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\"],\n  **{\n    \"s3.endpoint\": os.environ[\"R2_ENDPOINT\"],\n    \"s3.access-key-id\": os.environ[\"R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\"],\n    \"s3.secret-access-key\": os.environ[\"R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\"],\n    \"s3.region\": \"auto\",\n  },\n)\n```\n\n---\n\n## Iceberg スキーマ詳細\n\n### テーブル識別子\n\n* **Catalog**: `r2` (RestCatalog)\n* **Namespace**: `spotify` *(TODO: 後で再検討)*\n* **Table**: `recently_played`\n* **Full identifier**: `r2.spotify.recently_played`\n\n### PyIceberg Schema 定義\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.schema import Schema\nfrom pyiceberg.types import (\n    NestedField, StructType, ListType,\n    StringType, IntegerType, LongType, BooleanType, TimestamptzType,\n)\n\nARTIST_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(101, \"id\",   StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(102, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(103, \"uri\",  StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(201, \"id\",           StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(202, \"name\",         StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(203, \"uri\",          StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(204, \"album_type\",   StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(205, \"release_date\", StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(206, \"total_tracks\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n)\n\nTRACK_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(301, \"id\",          StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(302, \"name\",        StringType(),  required=True),\n    NestedField(303, \"uri\",         StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(304, \"duration_ms\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(305, \"explicit\",    BooleanType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(306, \"popularity\",  IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(307, \"isrc\",        StringType(),  required=False),\n    NestedField(308, \"album\",       ALBUM_STRUCT,  required=False),\n    NestedField(\n        field_id=309, name=\"artists\",\n        field_type=ListType(\n            element_id=310,\n            element_type=ARTIST_STRUCT,\n            element_required=True,\n        ),\n        required=True,\n    ),\n)\n\nCONTEXT_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(401, \"type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(402, \"uri\",  StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nRECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA = Schema(\n    NestedField(1, \"played_at\",    TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(2, \"played_at_ms\", LongType(),        required=True),\n    NestedField(3, \"track\",        TRACK_STRUCT,      required=True),\n    NestedField(4, \"context\",      CONTEXT_STRUCT,    required=False),\n    NestedField(5, \"_raw_json\",    StringType(),      required=True),\n    NestedField(6, \"_ingested_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    identifier_field_ids=[1, 301],\n)\n```\n\n### 設計判断のメモ\n\n* `played_at_ms: long` を冗長に持つ理由: Spotify の `after=` パラメータは epoch ms を要求する。dbt 側や BI 側から直接取り出しやすくするため保持。\n* `album.release_date: string` の理由: Spotify 仕様で `YYYY` / `YYYY-MM` / `YYYY-MM-DD` が混在するため、`DateType` では型不一致で落ちる。dbt 側で `release_date_precision` と組み合わせて正規化。\n* `artists: list<struct>` を `required=True`: Spotify 仕様上必ず 1 件以上ある。\n* `_raw_json: required=True`: 保険列として必須化。将来スキーマに無い新フィールドも dbt で拾える。\n* `identifier_field_ids`: 論理 PK の宣言用。PyIceberg の append では強制されないが、後で MERGE INTO で活用できる。\n\n### Partition Spec\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.partitioning import PartitionSpec, PartitionField\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import DayTransform\n\nPARTITION_SPEC = PartitionSpec(\n    PartitionField(\n        source_id=1, field_id=1000,\n        transform=DayTransform(), name=\"played_at_day\",\n    ),\n)\n```\n\n### Sort Order\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.table.sorting import SortOrder, SortField\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import IdentityTransform\n\nSORT_ORDER = SortOrder(\n    SortField(source_id=1, transform=IdentityTransform()),\n)\n```\n\n### テーブル初期化フロー\n\n```python\ndef ensure_table() -> Table:\n    if not catalog.table_exists((\"spotify\", \"recently_played\")):\n        catalog.create_namespace_if_not_exists(\"spotify\")\n        catalog.create_table(\n            identifier=(\"spotify\", \"recently_played\"),\n            schema=RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA,\n            partition_spec=PARTITION_SPEC,\n            sort_order=SORT_ORDER,\n            properties={\n                \"write.format.default\": \"parquet\",\n                \"write.parquet.compression-codec\": \"zstd\",\n                \"format-version\": \"2\",\n            },\n        )\n    return catalog.load_table((\"spotify\", \"recently_played\"))\n```\n\n初回実行時に自動作成される。手動 bootstrap 不要。\n\n---\n\n## データフロー・差分管理・エラーハンドリング\n\n### 差分管理カーソル（正常系）\n\nSpotify `recently-played` の `after` パラメータ仕様: epoch ms、strict `>`、新しい順に最大 50 件。\n\nKV に `cursor = <最後に取り込んだ played_at_ms>` を保存 → 次回は `after=<cursor>` で厳密に差分。\n\n### 初回ブートストラップ\n\n**方針**: `cursor` 未設定なら `after=<1時間前>` を送る。\n\n```python\nFIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000  # 1 hour\n\ncursor = await state.get(\"cursor\")\nif cursor is None:\n    after_ms = int(time.time() * 1000) - FIRST_RUN_LOOKBACK_MS\nelse:\n    after_ms = int(cursor)\n```\n\n過去履歴が欲しい場合は Spotify Streaming History JSON を別途申請・手動投入（Phase 1 スコープ外、TODO）。\n\n### エラー分類と対処\n\n**Transient（自動リトライ）**:\n\n| 種別 | 判定 | 対処 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| Spotify 5xx | HTTP 500–599 | exponential backoff 3 回（0.5s, 1s, 2s） |\n| Spotify 429 | HTTP 429 | `Retry-After` ヘッダに従う（上限 30s） |\n| R2 Catalog/S3 5xx | HTTP 5xx | pyiceberg 内の httpx/boto3 のリトライに任せる |\n| ネットワーク瞬断 | `ConnectionError` | exponential backoff 3 回 |\n\n**Permanent（ログ + 停止、cursor 据え置き）**:\n\n| 種別 | 判定 | 対処 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| refresh_token 失効 | Spotify `invalid_grant` | CRITICAL ログ + 例外 raise、再認可が必要 |\n| Spotify scope 不足 | 403 + `insufficient_scope` | 同上 |\n| R2 Catalog 認証失敗 | 401/403 | CRITICAL ログ + 例外 raise |\n| Iceberg schema 不整合 | pyarrow cast 失敗 | 1 件ずつ処理、失敗分は `_dlq/` へ JSON 退避 |\n\n### ハーフ成功（冪等性）\n\n**危険なケース**: Iceberg commit 成功 → KV cursor 更新失敗。次回 cron で重複取得。\n\n**Phase 1 方針**: ベストエフォート、**重複は dbt 側で dedup 吸収**。`stg_spotify__recently_played` で `QUALIFY ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY played_at, track.id ORDER BY _ingested_at DESC) = 1`。\n\n### シーケンス図\n\n```\nWorker.scheduled          Container /run         KV(outbound)        Spotify           R2/Iceberg\n     │                         │                      │                 │                  │\n     │─── cron fire ──────────>│                      │                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET /refresh_tok ─>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<──── 200 OK ─────────│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── POST /api/token ──────────────────── │                  │\n     │                         │<─── access_token ────────────────────  │                  │\n     │                         │   (refresh_token rotated?)             │                  │\n     │                         │── PUT /refresh_tok ─>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 204 ─────────────│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET /cursor ──────>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 200 \"17135...\" ──│                 │                  │\n     │                         │── GET recently-played?after=... ────── │                  │\n     │                         │<─── items[] ─────────────────────────  │                  │\n     │                         │  build pyarrow.Table                   │                  │\n     │                         │── table.append(arrow) ─────────────────────────────────── │\n     │                         │<───────────────── snapshot_id ──────────────────────────── │\n     │                         │── PUT /cursor ──────>│                 │                  │\n     │                         │<─── 204 ─────────────│                 │                  │\n     │<─── 200 {rows: N} ──────│                      │                 │                  │\n```\n\n### 観測性\n\n* **Worker**: `console.log` で container 呼び出し結果（Workers Observability で自動収集）\n* **Container**: Python `logging` を JSON 形式で stdout へ\n* **主要ログフィールド**: `run_id` (UUID), `rows_written`, `new_cursor`, `snapshot_id`, `spotify_api_latency_ms`, `iceberg_commit_latency_ms`\n* **アラート**: `invalid_grant` / 認証失敗は `log level=ERROR` + `alert=true` フィールド（通知は TODO）\n\n---\n\n## 開発・テスト・デプロイ\n\n### セットアップ手順（初回 1 回のみ）\n\n```\n1. R2 Data Catalog 有効化\n     wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n\n2. Spotify Developer アプリ作成\n     https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard\n     Redirect URI: http://localhost:8888/callback\n\n3. refresh_token を手元で取得\n     uv run python scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py\n\n4. KV namespace 作成\n     wrangler kv namespace create SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\n\n5. 初期値を KV に投入\n     wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV \\\n       \"refresh_token\" \"<手順3の値>\"\n\n6. Container 側 secrets 登録（7本）\n     wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID\n     wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_URI\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\n     wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\n     wrangler secret put R2_ENDPOINT\n     wrangler secret put R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\n     wrangler secret put R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\n\n7. デプロイ\n     cd spotify-iceberg && pnpm wrangler deploy\n\n8. 初回トリガ（手動確認）\n     wrangler tail で cron 発火を観察\n```\n\n**必要な Spotify scope**: `user-read-recently-played`\n\n### `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py`\n\n```python\nimport spotipy\nfrom spotipy.oauth2 import SpotifyOAuth\n\nauth = SpotifyOAuth(\n    client_id=input(\"Client ID: \"),\n    client_secret=input(\"Client Secret: \"),\n    redirect_uri=\"http://localhost:8888/callback\",\n    scope=\"user-read-recently-played\",\n    open_browser=True,\n    cache_path=None,\n)\ntoken_info = auth.get_access_token(as_dict=True)\nprint(f\"refresh_token = {token_info['refresh_token']}\")\n```\n\n### テスト戦略\n\n**Python ユニット** (`container/tests/`):\n\n| ファイル | 対象 | 手法 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| `test_schema.py` | `schema.py` | fixture の pyarrow Table で cast 確認 |\n| `test_spotify.py` | `spotify.py` | `responses` で Spotify API モック、refresh rotate / 0件 / 50件 / 429 網羅 |\n| `test_iceberg_writer.py` | `iceberg_writer.py` | SQLite-backed `SqlCatalog` + local fs で本物の R2 に触らず検証 |\n| `test_pipeline.py` | `pipeline.py` | 全統合、httpx で outbound KV もモック |\n\n**Worker ユニット** (`worker/src/__tests__/`):\n\n* `outbound.test.ts`: `kv.internal` が GET/PUT に正応答、未知ホストは 404\n\n**E2E 手動**:\n\n1. `wrangler tail` で cron 発火ログを観察\n2. DuckDB から読み取り:\n\n   ```sql\n   INSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;\n   ATTACH '<R2_CATALOG_URI>' AS r2 (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT_TYPE S3_TABLES);\n   SELECT played_at, track.name, track.artists[1].name\n   FROM r2.spotify.recently_played\n   ORDER BY played_at DESC LIMIT 10;\n   ```\n\n**CI（GitHub Actions）**:\n\n* `pytest container/`、`pnpm -C worker test:run`\n* Lint: `biome check worker/`, `ruff check container/`\n\n### デプロイ運用\n\n| 場面 | 操作 |\n| -- | -- |\n| コード変更 | PR → CI pass → main merge → Workers Builds が自動デプロイ |\n| secret rotation | `wrangler secret put` |\n| KV 手動修正 | `wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV cursor <new_ms>` |\n| schema 追加 | コード変更 → deploy、Iceberg が自動進化（append 時） |\n| ロールバック | Worker は dashboard から前リビジョンへ |\n\n---\n\n## 実装フェーズ分割\n\n| Phase | 内容 | 成果物 |\n| -- | -- | -- |\n| **1.1** | プロジェクトスキャフォールド（worker/, container/, Dockerfile, wrangler.jsonc） | deploy 可能な空の Worker + Container |\n| **1.2** | 手元 refresh token 取得スクリプト + KV セットアップ検証 | `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py` で取得可能 |\n| **1.3** | Container に Spotify 呼び出し実装（Iceberg 切り離しスタブ） | `/run` POST で items 件数を返す |\n| **1.4** | PyIceberg 書き込み + テーブル自動作成 | 実データが Iceberg に書かれ DuckDB で読める |\n| **1.5** | 差分管理（cursor）+ Worker cron 配線 + outbound KV ハンドラ | 毎時 cron で実データが積み上がる |\n| **1.6** | エラーハンドリング + ログ整備 + テスト | CI green、本番運用開始 |\n\nPhase 2 以降（別 issue）: `top_tracks_daily`, `saved_tracks`, `audio_features` enrichment, Streaming History bulk backfill, Workflows 化。\n\n---\n\n## Open Questions（TODO）\n\n- [ ] Container の `sleepAfter` — 初期値 5m、実運用で Worker 起動時間を見て調整\n- [ ] 将来 Cloudflare Workflows に巻き取るか — retry/observability と複雑さの天秤\n- [ ] Iceberg namespace 名 \"spotify\" 妥当性 — 他プロジェクトとの整合性\n- [ ] 過去履歴バックフィル — Spotify Streaming History JSON からの一括 append スクリプト\n- [ ] Iceberg commit 失敗時の dead-letter — items を R2 に JSON 退避\n- [ ] アラート通知 — `invalid_grant` / 認証失敗時の Slack or メール通知\n- [ ] secret を Cloudflare Secrets Store に移行するか\n\n---\n\n## 成功基準\n\n* 毎時の cron で Iceberg テーブルに新しい再生履歴が追記される\n* DuckDB から `SELECT * FROM r2.spotify.recently_played` でクエリできる\n* dbt（`modeling/`）から stg モデルとして flatten 可能な構造になっている\n* 1 週間エラーなしで稼働する\n* Container の月間コストが $1 未満に収まる（毎時起動 × 数秒実行）","priority":{"value":3,"name":"Medium"},"url":"https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469/spotify-r2-iceberg-データパイプライン設計","gitBranchName":"feature/ta-469","createdAt":"2026-04-19T16:12:30.912Z","updatedAt":"2026-04-19T16:12:30.912Z","archivedAt":null,"completedAt":null,"startedAt":null,"canceledAt":null,"dueDate":null,"slaStartedAt":null,"slaMediumRiskAt":null,"slaHighRiskAt":null,"slaBreachesAt":null,"slaType":"all","status":"Backlog","statusType":"backlog","labels":["iceberg","data-ingestion","cloudflare","Feature"],"attachments":[],"documents":[],"createdBy":"Takumi   "content": "# Spotify → R2 Iceberg Implementation Plan\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Spec:** [Linear TA-469](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469/spotify-r2-iceberg-データパイプライン設計)\n\n**Goal:** Build a Cloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that pulls Spotify recently-played history every hour and appends it to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog using PyIceberg.\n\n**Architecture:** A thin TypeScript Worker receives the hourly cron, starts a Durable-Object-backed Container, and exposes the `SPOTIFY_STATE_KV` binding to the container via `outboundByHost` virtual hosts. The container (Python + FastAPI) orchestrates Spotify OAuth refresh, API polling with a `played_at_ms` cursor, PyArrow table construction, and a single `table.append()` commit to the Iceberg REST catalog. Transformation is left to dbt on Databricks — the Iceberg layer is raw-nested with a `_raw_json` safety column.\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.7, `@cloudflare/containers` 0.2+, Hono, Vitest, Python 3.12, FastAPI, Uvicorn, `spotipy`, `pyiceberg[pyarrow,s3fs]`, `pyarrow`, `httpx`, pytest, uv, Biome, ruff.\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\nspotify-iceberg/\n├── README.md\n├── worker/\n│   ├──  ├── spotify.py        # Spotify API client\n│   │       ├── schema.py         # PyIceberg Schema + PartitionSpec\n│   │       ├── state.py          # KV outbound read/write (httpx)\n│   │       ├── get_refresh_token.py      # one-shot local OAuth helper\n```\n\nEach file has one responsibility:\n\n- `worker/src/index.ts` — Cloudflare runtime entrypoints (scheduled, outbound, DO class)\n-  \"@cloudflare/vitest-pool-workers\": \"^0.8.0\",\n    \"@cloudflare/workers-types\": \"^4.20260301.0\",\n    \"typescript\": \"^5.7.0\",\n    \"vitest\": \"~1.5.0\",\n    \"wrangler\": \"^4.4.0\"\n wiring**\n\n```typescript\nimport { Container } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\nexport class SpotifyContainer extends Container<Env> {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\";\n}\n\nSpotifyContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"kv.internal\": async (request, env: Env): Promise<Response> => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1);\n    if (!key) return new Response(\"missing key\", { status: 400 });\n\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const value = await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get(key);\n      if (value === null) return new Response(\"\", { status: 404 });\n      return new Response(value, { status: 200 });\n    }\n\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      const body = await request.text();\n      await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put(key, body);\n      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"method not allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nexport default {\n  async scheduled(_event, env, _ctx) {\n    const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n    const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n    const res = await stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    console.log(`container /run status=${res.status}`);\n  },\n  async fetch(request, env) {\n    if (new URL(request.url).pathname === \"/trigger\") {\n      const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n      const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n      return stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    }\n    return new Response(\"spotify-iceberg worker\", { status: 200 });\n  },\n} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Typecheck**\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker\npnpm typecheck\n```\n\nExpected: no errors.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd ../..\ngit add spotify-iceberg/worker/src/index.ts\ngit commit -m \"feat(spotify-iceberg): add outbound KV handler and scheduled trigger\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task A5: Wrangler config with Container + DO + cron\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/worker/wrangler.jsonc`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create `spotify-iceberg/worker/wrangler.jsonc`**\n\n```jsonc\n{\n  \"$schema\": \"node_modules/wrangler/config-schema.json\",\n  \"name\": \"spotify-iceberg\",\n  \"main\": \"src/index.ts\",\n  \"compatibility_date\": \"2026-04-20\",\n  \"compatibility_flags\": [\"nodejs_compat\"],\n  \"observability\": {\n    \"enabled\": true,\n    \"head_sampling_rate\": 1\n  },\n  \"triggers\": {\n    \"crons\": [\"0 * * * *\"]\n  },\n  \"kv_namespaces\": [\n    {\n      \"binding\": \"SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\",\n      \"id\": \"REPLACE_WITH_WRANGLER_KV_CREATE_OUTPUT\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"containers\": [\n    {\n      \"class_name\": \"SpotifyContainer\",\n      \"image\": \"../container/Dockerfile\",\n (one-shot)\n\n### Task B1: Local `get_refresh_token.py`\n\nThis script runs locally once to obtain a `refresh_token` via the Authorization Code flow. No unit test; verification is the interactive flow.\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/scripts/get_refresh_token.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create the script**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nOne-shot helper to obtain a Spotify refresh_token for user-read-recently-played.\n\nUsage:\n  uv run --with spotipy python spotify-iceberg/scripts/get_refresh_token.py\n\nPrerequisite: in https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard register an app with\nredirect URI http://localhost:8888/callback.\n\nAfter printing the refresh_token, load it into KV:\n  wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token \"<value>\"\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nimport spotipy\nfrom spotipy.oauth2 import SpotifyOAuth\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    client_id = input(\"Spotify Client ID: \").strip()\n    client_secret = input(\"Spotify Client Secret: \").strip()\n    if not client_id or not client_secret:\n        sys.exit(\"client id/secret required\")\n\n    auth = SpotifyOAuth(\n        client_id=client_id,\n        client_secret=client_secret,\n        redirect_uri=\"http://localhost:8888/callback\",\n        scope=\"user-read-recently-played\",\n        open_browser=True,\n        cache_path=None,\n    )\n    token_info = auth.get_access_token(as_dict=True)\n    refresh_token = token_info.get(\"refresh_token\")\n    if not refresh_token:\n        sys.exit(\"no refresh_token returned; check scope grant\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== refresh_token ===\")\n    print(refresh_token)\n    print(\"\\nLoad into KV:\")\n    print(\n        f'  wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token \"{refresh_token}\"'\n    )\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/scripts/get_refresh_token.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(spotify-iceberg): add local refresh_token helper script\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase C — PyIceberg schema\n\n### Task C1: Schema module with types, partition spec, sort order\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/schema.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create schema module**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nIceberg schema for the spotify.recently_played table.\n\nThe schema mirrors the Spotify API item structure (fully nested) with two\naudit columns appended. Transformation/flatten is deferred to dbt.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pyiceberg.partitioning import PartitionField, PartitionSpec\nfrom pyiceberg.schema import Schema\nfrom pyiceberg.table.sorting import SortField, SortOrder\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import DayTransform, IdentityTransform\nfrom pyiceberg.types import (\n    BooleanType,\n    IntegerType,\n    ListType,\n    LongType,\n    NestedField,\n    StringType,\n    StructType,\n    TimestamptzType,\n)\n\n# --- artist ---\nARTIST_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(101, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(102, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(103, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\n# --- album ---\nALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(201, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(202, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(203, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(204, \"album_type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(205, \"release_date\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(206, \"total_tracks\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n)\n\n# --- track ---\nTRACK_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(301, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(302, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(303, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(304, \"duration_ms\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(305, \"explicit\", BooleanType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(306, \"popularity\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(307, \"isrc\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(308, \"album\", ALBUM_STRUCT, required=False),\n    NestedField(\n        field_id=309,\n        name=\"artists\",\n        field_type=ListType(\n            element_id=310,\n            element_type=ARTIST_STRUCT,\n            element_required=True,\n        ),\n        required=True,\n    ),\n)\n\n# --- context ---\nCONTEXT_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(401, \"type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(402, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nRECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA = Schema(\n    NestedField(1, \"played_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(2, \"played_at_ms\", LongType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(3, \"track\", TRACK_STRUCT, required=True),\n    NestedField(4, \"context\", CONTEXT_STRUCT, required=False),\n    NestedField(5, \"_raw_json\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(6, \"_ingested_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    identifier_field_ids=[1, 301],\n)\n\nPARTITION_SPEC = PartitionSpec(\n    PartitionField(\n        source_id=1,\n        field_id=1000,\n        transform=DayTransform(),\n        name=\"played_at_day\",\n    ),\n)\n\nSORT_ORDER = SortOrder(\n    SortField(source_id=1, transform=IdentityTransform()),\n)\n\nTABLE_PROPERTIES = {\n    \"write.format.default\": \"parquet\",\n    \"write.parquet.compression-codec\": \"zstd\",\n    \"format-version\": \"2\",\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/schema.py\ngit commit -m \"feat(spotify-iceberg): define Iceberg schema for recently_played\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task C2: Schema test fixture + round-trip test\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/container/tests/fixtures/recently_played_sample.json`\n- Create: dataclasses import dataclass\n\nimport httpx\n\nTOKEN_URL = \"https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token\"\nAPI_BASE = \"https://api.spotify.com/v1\"\n\n\nclass SpotifyError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Base exception for Spotify API failures.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass InvalidGrantError(SpotifyError):\n    \"\"\"The refresh_token is no longer valid; human re-authorization needed.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass TokenResult:\n    access_token: str\n    refresh_token: str\n\n\nclass SpotifyClient:\n    \"\"\"Thin HTTP client for Spotify. Stateless.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        client_id: str,\n        client_secret: str,\n        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._client_id = client_id\n        self._client_secret = client_secret\n        self._http = http_client or httpx.Client(timeout=30.0)\n\n    def _basic_auth(self) -> str:\n        raw = f\"{self._client_id}:{self._client_secret}\".encode()\n        return \"Basic \" + base64.b64encode(raw).decode()\n\n    def refresh_access_token(self, refresh_token: str) -> TokenResult:\n        resp = self._http.post(\n            TOKEN_URL,\n            headers={\n                \"Authorization\": self._basic_auth(),\n                \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\",\n            },\n            data={\"grant_type\": \"refresh_token\", \"refresh_token\": refresh_token},\n        )\n        if resp.status_code == 400:\n resp.status_code >= 400:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"token endpoint {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\")\n\n        body = resp.json()\n        return TokenResult(\n `spotify.py` with a retry helper**\n\nReplace the `recently_played` implementation with:\n\n```python\nimport time\n\nMAX_RETRIES = 3\nBACKOFF_BASE_SEC = 0.5\n\n\ndef _should_retry(status_code: int) -> bool:\n    return status_code == 429 or 500 <= status_code < 600\n\n\ndef _retry_after_sec(response: httpx.Response) -> float:\n    header = response.headers.get(\"Retry-After\")\n    if header is not None:\n        try:\n            return min(float(header), 30.0)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    return 0.0\n\n\nclass SpotifyClient:\n    # ... existing __init__ / _basic_auth / refresh_access_token ...\n\n    def recently_played(\n        self,\n        access_token: str,\n        after_ms: int,\n        limit: int = 50,\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        url = f\"{API_BASE}/me/player/recently-played\"\n        params = {\"after\": after_ms, \"limit\": limit}\n        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {access_token}\"}\n\n        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES + 1):\n            resp = self._http.get(url, params=params, headers=headers)\n            if not _should_retry(resp.status_code):\n                break\n            if attempt == MAX_RETRIES:\n                raise SpotifyError(\n                    f\"recently-played failed after {MAX_RETRIES + 1} attempts: \"\n                    f\"status={resp.status_code}\"\n                )\n            sleep_sec = _retry_after_sec(resp) or BACKOFF_BASE_SEC * (2**attempt)\n            time.sleep(sleep_sec)\n\n        if resp.status_code == 401:\n            raise SpotifyError(\n                \"spotify returned 401; access_token likely expired mid-flight\"\n            )\n        if resp.status_code == 403:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"spotify forbidden: {resp.text}\")\n        if resp.status_code >= 400:\n            raise SpotifyError(\n                f\"recently-played {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\"\n            )\n        return resp.json().get(\"items\", [])\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run — expect PASS**\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/test_spotify.py -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd ../..\ngit add      self,\n        base_url: str,\n        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n        self._http = http_client or missing)**\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Add to `iceberg_writer.py`**\n\n```python\nfrom pyiceberg.catalog import Catalog\nfrom pyiceberg.table import Table\n\nfrom .schema import PARTITION_SPEC, RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA, SORT_ORDER, TABLE_PROPERTIES\n\n\ndef ensure_table(catalog: Catalog, identifier: tuple[str, str]) -> Table:\n    namespace, _ = identifier\n    if (namespace,) not in catalog.list_namespaces():\n        catalog.create_namespace(namespace)\n    try:\n        return catalog.load_table(identifier)\n    except Exception:\n        return catalog.create_table(\n            identifier=identifier,\n            schema=RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA,\n            partition_spec=PARTITION_SPEC,\n            sort_order=SORT_ORDER,\n            properties=TABLE_PROPERTIES,\n        )\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run — expect PASS**\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/test_iceberg_writer.py -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd ../..\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container\ngit \"refresh_token not found in KV — run scripts/get_refresh_token.py \"\n            \"and load the value with `wrangler kv key put`\"\n        )\n\n    token = cursor_str is None:\n        after_ms = int(now().timestamp() * 1000) - first_run_lookback_ms\n    else:\n        after_ms = int(cursor_str)\n\n    items = spotify.recently_played(token.access_token, after_ms=after_ms)\n    if not items:\n        return PipelineResult(rows_written=0, new_cursor_ms=None, snapshot_id=None)\n\n    append_result: AppendResult = append_items(\n        catalog,\n        TABLE_IDENTIFIER,\n        items,\n        ingested_at=now(),\n    )\n\n    latest_ms = max(_played_at_ms(item) for item in items)\n    state.put(\"cursor\", str(latest_ms))\n\n    return PipelineResult(\n        rows_written=append_result.rows_written,\n        new_cursor_ms=latest_ms,\n        snapshot_id=append_result.snapshot_id,\n    )\n\n\ndef _played_at_ms(item: dict) -> int:\n    ts = item[\"played_at\"]\n    if ts.endswith(\"Z\"):\n        ts = ts[:-1] + \"+00:00\"\n    return int(datetime.fromisoformat(ts).timestamp() * 1000)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run — expect = resp.json()\n    assert body[\"rows_written\"] == 3\n    assert body[\"new_cursor_ms\"] == 1713500400000\n    assert body[\"snapshot_id\"] == 4242\n\n\ndef test_run_surfaces_errors_as_500() -> None:\n    with patch(\"spotify_iceberg.main.run_recently_played\", side_effect=RuntimeError(\"boom\")):\n        client = TestClient(app)\n        resp = client.post(\"/run\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 500\n    assert \"boom\" in resp.json()[\"error\"]\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run — expect FAIL**\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `main.py`**\n\n```python\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom dataclasses import asdict\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom pyiceberg.catalog.rest import RestCatalog\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .pipeline import run_recently_played\nfrom .spotify import SpotifyClient\nfrom .state import StateStore\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"spotify_iceberg\")\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(message)s\")\n\napp = FastAPI(title=\"spotify-iceberg\")\n\n\ndef _build_catalog(settings: Settings) -> RestCatalog:\n    return RestCatalog(\n        \"r2\",\n        **{\n            \"uri\": settings.r2_catalog_uri,\n            \"token\": settings.r2_catalog_token,\n            \"warehouse\": settings.r2_catalog_warehouse,\n            \"s3.endpoint\": settings.r2_endpoint,\n            \"s3.access-key-id\": settings.r2_access_key_id,\n            \"s3.secret-access-key\": settings.r2_secret_access_key,\n            \"s3.region\": \"auto\",\n        },\n    )\n\n\n@app.get(\"/health\")\ndef health() -> dict[str, str]:\n    return {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n\n\n@app.post(\"/run\")\ndef run() -> JSONResponse:\n    settings = Settings()\n    state = StateStore(base_url=settings.kv_base_url)\n    spotify = SpotifyClient(\n        client_id=settings.spotify_client_id,\n        client_secret=settings.spotify_client_secret,\n    )\n    catalog = _build_catalog(settings)\n\n    try:\n        result = run_recently_played(\n            state=state,\n            spotify=spotify,\n            catalog=catalog,\n            first_run_lookback_ms=settings.first_run_lookback_ms,\n        )\n    except Exception as exc:\n        logger.exception(\"pipeline failed\")\n        return JSONResponse({\"error\": str(exc)}, status_code=500)\n\n    logger.info(\"pipeline ok rows=%s cursor=%s\", result.rows_written, result.new_cursor_ms)\n    return JSONResponse(asdict(result))\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run — expect PASS**\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/container && uv run pytest tests/test_main_run.py -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ncd ../..\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container\ngit commit -m \"feat(spotify-iceberg): FastAPI /run endpoint wires pipeline\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase I — Deployment prep\n\n### Task I1: Container README with setup instructions\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/README.md`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write README**\n\n```markdown\n# spotify-iceberg\n\nCloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that appends Spotify recently-played\nhistory to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog every hour.\n\nSpec: [Linear TA-469](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469/spotify-r2-iceberg-データパイプライン設計)\n\n## Setup (one-time)\n\n### 1. Enable R2 Data Catalog\n\n```bash\nwrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n```\n\nNote the printed **Catalog URI** and **Warehouse** — you'll need them in step 6.\n\n### 2. Create Spotify Developer app\n\nAt <https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard>, create an app and register\n`http://localhost:8888/callback` as a Redirect URI. Note the Client ID and\nClient Secret.\n\n### 3. Obtain refresh_token locally\n\n```bash\nuv run --with spotipy python spotify-iceberg/scripts/get_refresh_token.py\n```\n\nFollow the browser flow; the script prints the refresh_token.\n\n### 4. Create KV namespace\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker\nwrangler kv namespace create SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\n```\n\nCopy the printed `id` into `wrangler.jsonc` under `kv_namespaces[0].id`.\n\n### 5. Store refresh_token in KV\n\n```bash\nwrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token \"<value-from-step-3>\"\n```\n\n### 6. Register secrets (from spotify-iceberg/worker/)\n\n```bash\nwrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID\nwrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_URI\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\nwrangler secret put R2_ENDPOINT\nwrangler secret put R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\nwrangler secret put R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\n```\n\n### 7. Deploy\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker && pnpm deploy\n```\n\n### 8. Verify\n\n- Trigger manually: `curl -X POST https://spotify-iceberg.<account>.workers.dev/trigger`\n- Tail logs: `wrangler tail`\n- Query via DuckDB:\n\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;\nATTACH '<R2_CATALOG_URI>' AS r2 (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT_TYPE S3_TABLES);\nSELECT played_at, track.name, track.artists[1].name\nFROM r2.spotify.recently_played\nORDER BY played_at DESC LIMIT 10;\n```\n\n## Development\n\n- Worker: `cd worker && pnpm test:run && pnpm typecheck && pnpm check`\n- Container: `cd container && uv run pytest && uv run ruff check .`\n\n## Open TODOs\n\nSee [TA-469 Open Questions](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469).\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/README.md\ngit commit -m \"docs(spotify-iceberg): setup and verification instructions\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task I2: Wrangler local dev smoke (no test; manual)\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Run local dev**\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker && pnpm dev\n```\n\nExpected:\n- Wrangler starts, builds container image\n- Visiting `http://localhost:8787/` returns `spotify-iceberg worker`\n- Visiting `http://localhost:8787/trigger` attempts to reach container (may fail at KV read — that's fine for smoke)\n\nStop with `Ctrl+C`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Deploy to production**\n\n```bash\npnpm deploy\n```\n\nExpected: Worker deploys without errors. Container image is built and uploaded.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: No commit needed**\n\n---\n\n### Task I3: E2E verification (manual, after setup steps 1–6 of README complete)\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Manually trigger**\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST https://spotify-iceberg.<acct>.workers.dev/trigger\n```\n\nExpected response: `{\"rows_written\": N, \"new_cursor_ms\": ..., \"snapshot_id\": ...}` (N may be 0 if no new plays).\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify KV cursor advanced**\n\n```bash\nwrangler kv key get --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV cursor\n```\n\nExpected: an epoch-ms integer corresponding to the latest played_at.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: DuckDB query**\n\nUse the query from README §8. Expected: at least N rows with matching track names.\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Wait for one cron firing (up to 1 hour) and re-check**\n\nExpected: log entry `container /run status=200` in `wrangler tail`; row count grows if new plays happened.\n\n---\n\n## Phase J — CI integration\n\n### Task J1: GitHub Actions for spotify-iceberg\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `.github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create workflow file**\n\n```yaml\nname: spotify-iceberg CI\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    paths:\n      - \"spotify-iceberg/**\"\n      - \".github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\"\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - \"spotify-iceberg/**\"\n      - \".github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\"\n\njobs:\n  worker:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    defaults:\n      run:\n        working-directory: spotify-iceberg/worker\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4\n        with:\n          version: 9\n      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4\n        with:\n          node-version: 20\n          cache: pnpm\n          cache-dependency-path: spotify-iceberg/worker/pnpm-lock.yaml\n      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile\n      - run: pnpm check\n      - run: pnpm typecheck\n      - run: pnpm test:run\n\n  container:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    defaults:\n      run:\n        working-directory: spotify-iceberg/container\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4\n        with:\n          version: latest\n      - run: uv sync --extra dev\n      - run: uv run ruff check .\n      - run: uv run pytest -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add .github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\ngit commit -m \"ci(spotify-iceberg): add worker+container CI workflow\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase K — Final polish\n\n### Task K1: Link plan and spec from Linear + update status\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Post comment on TA-469 linking to the implemented branch/PR**\n\n(Do this manually or with the Linear MCP after the PR is opened.)\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Move TA-469 to \"In Progress\" when Task A1 starts, \"In Review\" when PR opens, \"Done\" when merged**\n\nNo code; Linear status transitions only.\n\n---\n\n## Full test matrix\n\nRun everything before declaring Phase 1 complete:\n\n```bash\n# Worker\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker\npnpm check\npnpm typecheck\npnpm test:run\n\n# Container\ncd ../container\nuv run ruff check .\nuv run pytest -v\n```\n\nExpected: all green.\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n- **6 phases A–K** (~24 tasks) producing a deployable hourly pipeline\n- **All non-interactive tasks follow TDD**: failing test → implement → passing test → commit\n- **No network in tests**: `respx` mocks Spotify API + KV outbound; `SqlCatalog` + local filesystem replaces R2\n- **Phase 2 (separate plan)**: `top_tracks_daily`, `saved_tracks`, `audio_features` enrichment, Streaming History bulk backfill, Workflows migration — all listed as Open Questions in TA-469\n"
}

## 実装計画を作成しました

**保存先**: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-04-20-spotify-r2-iceberg.md`

### 計画の構成

- **Phase A — Project scaffold** (Task A1–A6): Worker + Container の雛形、outbound KV ハンドラ、wrangler.jsonc
- **Phase B — refresh token helper** (Task B1): `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py`
- **Phase C — PyIceberg schema** (Task C1–C2): 完全ネスト + `_raw_json` + day partition
- **Phase D — Spotify API client** (Task D1–D4): Settings, `refresh_access_token`, `recently_played`, 429/5xx リトライ
- **Phase E — KV state via outbound** (Task E1): `StateStore.get/put`
- **Phase F — Iceberg writer** (Task F1–F3): `build_arrow_table`, `ensure_table`, `append_items`（SqlCatalog fixture でローカル完結）
- **Phase G — Pipeline orchestration** (Task G1–G2): happy path / first-run / 0件 / rotate / missing token
- **Phase H — FastAPI wiring** (Task H1): `/run` エンドポイント + エラー→500
- **Phase I — Deployment prep** (Task I1–I3): README、local dev smoke、E2E 手動確認
- **Phase J — CI** (Task J1): GitHub Actions（worker + container）
- **Phase K — Linear status 更新** (Task K1)

### セルフレビュー結果

- ✅ **spec カバレッジ**: TA-469 の全セクションにタスク対応あり
- ✅ **プレースホルダなし**: 全ステップに実コード/実コマンド
- ✅ **型整合性**: `TokenResult`, `AppendResult`, `PipelineResult`, `StateStore`, `SpotifyClient` が定義と使用で一貫
- ✅ **TDD 徹底**: 各実装タスクが failing test → 実装 → passing test → commit

全タスクは 2–5 分粒度。合計 ~24 タスク、phase ごとに独立して commit/レビュー可能。

---

## 実行方式を選んでください

**1. Subagent-Driven（推奨）** — タスクごとに fresh subagent を起動、間に2段階レビュー、並列性高く品質安定

**2. Inline Execution** — このセッションでそのまま実行、checkpoint 単位でレビュー

**どちらで進めますか？**（もしくは、計画自体を先にレビューしたい場合はそう伝えてください — その場合はファイルを直接見てから判断で OK）

1	# Spotify → R2 Iceberg Implementation Plan
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Spec:** [Linear TA-469](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469/spotify-r2-iceberg-データパイプライン設計)
6	
7	**Goal:** Build a Cloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that pulls Spotify recently-played history every hour and appends it to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog using PyIceberg.
8	
9	**Architecture:** A thin TypeScript Worker receives the hourly cron, starts a Durable-Object-backed Container, and exposes the `SPOTIFY_STATE_KV` binding to the container via `outboundByHost` virtual hosts. The container (Python + FastAPI) orchestrates Spotify OAuth refresh, API polling with a `played_at_ms` cursor, PyArrow table construction, and a single `table.append()` commit to the Iceberg REST catalog. Transformation is left to dbt on Databricks — the Iceberg layer is raw-nested with a `_raw_json` safety column.
10	
11	**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.7, `@cloudflare/containers` 0.2+, Hono, Vitest, Python 3.12, FastAPI, Uvicorn, `spotipy`, `pyiceberg[pyarrow,s3fs]`, `pyarrow`, `httpx`, pytest, uv, Biome, ruff.
12	
13	---
14

39	│   │       ├── pipeline.py       # run_recently_played()
40	│   │       ├── spotify.py        # Spotify API client
41	│   │       ├── schema.py         # PyIceberg Schema + PartitionSpec
42	│   │       ├── state.py          # KV outbound read/write (httpx)
43	│   │       ├── iceberg_writer.py # pyarrow → pyiceberg append
44	│   │       └── config.py         # env var loading
45	│   └── tests/

57	```
58	
59	Each file has one responsibility:
60	
61	- `worker/src/index.ts` — Cloudflare runtime entrypoints (scheduled, outbound, DO class)
62	- `container/src/spotify_iceberg/spotify.py` — HTTP against Spotify only; no Iceberg, no state
63	- `container/src/spotify_iceberg/iceberg_writer.py` — PyArrow+PyIceberg only; no Spotify, no KV
64	- `container/src/spotify_iceberg/state.py` — KV via outbound HTTP; no business logic
65	- `container/src/spotify_iceberg/pipeline.py` — glue between the three; retryable error boundaries
66	- `container/src/spotify_iceberg/main.py` — FastAPI shell; calls pipeline
67

388	### Task A4: Worker outbound handler scaffold (no test yet)
389	
390	**Files:**
391	- Modify: `spotify-iceberg/worker/src/index.ts`
392	
393	- [ ] **Step 1: Replace `src/index.ts` with outbound wiring**
394	
395	```typescript
396	import { Container } from "@cloudflare/containers";
397	
398	export class SpotifyContainer extends Container<Env> {
399	  defaultPort = 8080;
400	  sleepAfter = "5m";
401	}
402	
403	SpotifyContainer.outboundByHost = {
404	  "kv.internal": async (request, env: Env): Promise<Response> => {
405	    const url = new URL(request.url);
406	    const key = url.pathname.slice(1);
407	    if (!key) return new Response("missing key", { status: 400 });
408	
409	    if (request.method === "GET") {
410	      const value = await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get(key);
411	      if (value === null) return new Response("", { status: 404 });
412	      return new Response(value, { status: 200 });
413	    }
414	
415	    if (request.method === "PUT") {
416	      const body = await request.text();
417	      await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put(key, body);
418	      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
419	    }
420	
421	    return new Response("method not allowed", { status: 405 });
422	  },
423	};
424	
425	export default {
426	  async scheduled(_event, env, _ctx) {
427	    const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName("spotify");
428	    const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);
429	    const res = await stub.fetch("http://container/run", { method: "POST" });
430	    console.log(`container /run status=${res.status}`);
431	  },
432	  async fetch(request, env) {
433	    if (new URL(request.url).pathname === "/trigger") {
434	      const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName("spotify");
435	      const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);
436	      return stub.fetch("http://container/run", { method: "POST" });
437	    }
438	    return new Response("spotify-iceberg worker", { status: 200 });
439	  },
440	} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;
441	```

528	### Task A6: Worker outbound handler unit test
529	
530	**Files:**
531	- Create: `spotify-iceberg/worker/src/__tests__/outbound.test.ts`
532	
533	- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing test**
534	
535	```typescript
536	import { env, SELF } from "cloudflare:test";
537	import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
538	import { SpotifyContainer } from "../index";
539	
540	// Access outbound handler directly — it's a static property, not an HTTP route
541	const handler = SpotifyContainer.outboundByHost?.["kv.internal"];
542	
543	describe("outbound kv.internal handler", () => {
544	  beforeEach(async () => {
545	    await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.delete("refresh_token");
546	    await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.delete("cursor");
547	  });
548	
549	  it("returns 404 when key missing", async () => {
550	    if (!handler) throw new Error("handler not registered");
551	    const res = await handler(
552	      new Request("http://kv.internal/refresh_token"),
553	      env as Env,
554	      {} as ExecutionContext
555	    );
556	    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
557	  });
558	
559	  it("returns stored value on GET", async () => {
560	    if (!handler) throw new Error("handler not registered");
561	    await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put("refresh_token", "AQB-xyz");
562	    const res = await handler(
563	      new Request("http://kv.internal/refresh_token"),
564	      env as Env,
565	      {} as ExecutionContext
566	    );
567	    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
568	    expect(await res.text()).toBe("AQB-xyz");
569	  });
570	
571	  it("writes and reads back with PUT", async () => {
572	    if (!handler) throw new Error("handler not registered");
573	    const putRes = await handler(
574	      new Request("http://kv.internal/cursor", {
575	        method: "PUT",
576	        body: "1713500400000",
577	      }),
578	      env as Env,
579	      {} as ExecutionContext
580	    );
581	    expect(putRes.status).toBe(204);
582	    expect(await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get("cursor")).toBe("1713500400000");
583	  });
584	
585	  it("rejects unknown method with 405", async () => {
586	    if (!handler) throw new Error("handler not registered");
587	    const res = await handler(
588	      new Request("http://kv.internal/refresh_token", { method: "DELETE" }),
589	      env as Env,
590	      {} as ExecutionContext
591	    );
592	    expect(res.status).toBe(405);
593	  });
594	
595	  it("rejects empty key with 400", async () => {
596	    if (!handler) throw new Error("handler not registered");
597	    const res = await handler(
598	      new Request("http://kv.internal/"),
599	      env as Env,
600	      {} as ExecutionContext
601	    );
602	    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
603	  });
604	});
