625	## Phase B — Spotify refresh token helper (one-shot)
626	
627	### Task B1: Local `get_refresh_token.py`
628	
629	This script runs locally once to obtain a `refresh_token` via the Authorization Code flow. No unit test; verification is the interactive flow.
630	
631	**Files:**
632	- Create: `scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py`
633	
634	- [ ] **Step 1: Create the script**
635	
636	```python
637	"""
638	One-shot helper to obtain a Spotify refresh_token for user-read-recently-played.
639	
640	Usage:
641	  uv run --with spotipy python scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py
642	
643	Prerequisite: in https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard register an app with
644	redirect URI http://localhost:8888/callback.
645	
646	After printing the refresh_token, load it into KV:
647	  wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token "<value>"
648	"""
649	from __future__ import annotations
650	
651	import sys
652	
653	import spotipy
654	from spotipy.oauth2 import SpotifyOAuth
655	
656	
657	def main() -> None:
658	    client_id = input("Spotify Client ID: ").strip()
659	    client_secret = input("Spotify Client Secret: ").strip()
660	    if not client_id or not client_secret:
661	        sys.exit("client id/secret required")
662	
663	    auth = SpotifyOAuth(
664	        client_id=client_id,
665	        client_secret=client_secret,
666	        redirect_uri="http://localhost:8888/callback",
667	        scope="user-read-recently-played",
668	        open_browser=True,
669	        cache_path=None,
670	    )
671	    token_info = auth.get_access_token(as_dict=True)
672	    refresh_token = token_info.get("refresh_token")
673	    if not refresh_token:
674	        sys.exit("no refresh_token returned; check scope grant")
675	
676	    print("\n=== refresh_token ===")
677	    print(refresh_token)
678	    print("\nLoad into KV:")
679	    print(
680	        f'  wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token "{refresh_token}"'
681	    )
682	
683	
684	if __name__ == "__main__":
685	    main()
686	```
687	
688	- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**
689	
690	```bash
691	git add scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py
692	git commit -m "feat(spotify-iceberg): add local refresh_token helper script"
693	```

  "body": "# 実装計画 (1/4): Phase A + B\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Build a Cloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that pulls Spotify recently-played history every hour and appends it to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog using PyIceberg.\n\n**Architecture:** A thin TypeScript Worker receives the hourly cron, starts a Durable-Object-backed Container, and exposes the `SPOTIFY_STATE_KV` binding to the container via `outboundByHost` virtual hosts. The container (Python + FastAPI) orchestrates Spotify OAuth refresh, API polling with a `played_at_ms` cursor, PyArrow table construction, and a single `table.append()` commit to the Iceberg REST catalog. Transformation is left to dbt on Databricks — the Iceberg layer is raw-nested with a `_raw_json` safety column.\n\n**Tech Stack:** TypeScript 5.7, `@cloudflare/containers` 0.2+, Hono, Vitest, Python 3.12, FastAPI, Uvicorn, `spotipy`, `pyiceberg[pyarrow,s3fs]`, `pyarrow`, `httpx`, pytest, uv, Biome, ruff.\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n```\nspotify-iceberg/\n├── README.md\n├── worker/\n│   ├──  ├── spotify.py        # Spotify API client\n│   │       ├── schema.py         # PyIceberg Schema + PartitionSpec\n│   │       ├── state.py          # KV outbound read/write (httpx)\n│   │       ├── `spotify-iceberg/worker/src/index.ts`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Replace `src/index.ts` with outbound wiring**\n\n```typescript\nimport { Container } from \"@cloudflare/containers\";\n\nexport class SpotifyContainer extends Container<Env> {\n  defaultPort = 8080;\n  sleepAfter = \"5m\";\n}\n\nSpotifyContainer.outboundByHost = {\n  \"kv.internal\": async (request, env: Env): Promise<Response> => {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const key = url.pathname.slice(1);\n    if (!key) return new Response(\"missing key\", { status: 400 });\n\n    if (request.method === \"GET\") {\n      const value = await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.get(key);\n      if (value === null) return new Response(\"\", { status: 404 });\n      return new Response(value, { status: 200 });\n    }\n\n    if (request.method === \"PUT\") {\n      const body = await request.text();\n      await env.SPOTIFY_STATE_KV.put(key, body);\n      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"method not allowed\", { status: 405 });\n  },\n};\n\nexport default {\n  async scheduled(_event, env, _ctx) {\n    const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n    const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n    const res = await stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    console.log(`container /run status=${res.status}`);\n  },\n  async fetch(request, env) {\n    if (new URL(request.url).pathname === \"/trigger\") {\n      const id = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.idFromName(\"spotify\");\n      const stub = env.SPOTIFY_CONTAINER.get(id);\n      return stub.fetch(\"http://container/run\", { method: \"POST\" });\n    }\n    return new Response(\"spotify-iceberg worker\", { status: 200 });\n  },\n} satisfies ExportedHandler<Env>;\n```\n\n- [ 707	"""
708	Iceberg schema for the spotify.recently_played table.
709	
710	The schema mirrors the Spotify API item structure (fully nested) with two
711	audit columns appended. Transformation/flatten is deferred to dbt.
712	"""
713	from __future__ import annotations
714	
715	from pyiceberg.partitioning import PartitionField, PartitionSpec
716	from pyiceberg.schema import Schema
717	from pyiceberg.table.sorting import SortField, SortOrder
718	from pyiceberg.transforms import DayTransform, IdentityTransform
719	from pyiceberg.types import (
720	    BooleanType,
721	    IntegerType,
722	    ListType,
723	    LongType,
724	    NestedField,
725	    StringType,
726	    StructType,
727	    TimestamptzType,
728	)
729	
730	# --- artist ---
731	ARTIST_STRUCT = StructType(
732	    NestedField(101, "id", StringType(), required=True),
733	    NestedField(102, "name", StringType(), required=True),
734	    NestedField(103, "uri", StringType(), required=False),
735	)
736	
737	# --- album ---
738	ALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(
739	    NestedField(201, "id", StringType(), required=True),
740	    NestedField(202, "name", StringType(), required=True),
741	    NestedField(203, "uri", StringType(), required=False),
742	    NestedField(204, "album_type", StringType(), required=False),
743	    NestedField(205, "release_date", StringType(), required=False),
744	    NestedField(206, "total_tracks", IntegerType(), required=False),
745	)
746	
747	# --- track ---
748	TRACK_STRUCT = StructType(
749	    NestedField(301, "id", StringType(), required=True),
750	    NestedField(302, "name", StringType(), required=True),
751	    NestedField(303, "uri", StringType(), required=False),
752	    NestedField(304, "duration_ms", IntegerType(), required=False),
753	    NestedField(305, "explicit", BooleanType(), required=False),
754	    NestedField(306, "popularity", IntegerType(), required=False),
755	    NestedField(307, "isrc", StringType(), required=False),
756	    NestedField(308, "album", ALBUM_STRUCT, required=False),
757	    NestedField(
758	        field_id=309,
759	        name="artists",
760	        field_type=ListType(
761	            element_id=310,
762	            element_type=ARTIST_STRUCT,
763	            element_required=True,
764	        ),
765	        required=True,
766	    ),
767	)
768	
769	# --- context ---
770	CONTEXT_STRUCT = StructType(
771	    NestedField(401, "type", StringType(), required=False),
772	    NestedField(402, "uri", StringType(), required=False),
773	)
774	
775	RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA = Schema(
776	    NestedField(1, "played_at", TimestamptzType(), required=True),
777	    NestedField(2, "played_at_ms", LongType(), required=True),
778	    NestedField(3, "track", TRACK_STRUCT, required=True),
779	    NestedField(4, "context", CONTEXT_STRUCT, required=False),
780	    NestedField(5, "_raw_json", StringType(), required=True),
781	    NestedField(6, "_ingested_at", TimestamptzType(), required=True),
782	    identifier_field_ids=[1, 301],
783	)
784	
785	PARTITION_SPEC = PartitionSpec(
786	    PartitionField(
787	        source_id=1,
788	        field_id=1000,
789	        transform=DayTransform(),
790	        name="played_at_day",
791	    ),
792	)
793	
794	SORT_ORDER = SortOrder(
795	    SortField(source_id=1, transform=IdentityTransform()),
796	)
797	
798	TABLE_PROPERTIES = {
799	    "write.format.default": "parquet",
800	    "write.parquet.compression-codec": "zstd",
801	    "format-version": "2",
802	}
803	```
804	
805	- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**
806

808	git add spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/schema.py
809	git commit -m "feat(spotify-iceberg): define Iceberg schema for recently_played"
810	```

1106	- [ ] **Step 3: Create `src/spotify_iceberg/spotify.py`**
1107	
1108	```python
1109	from __future__ import annotations
1110	
1111	import base64
1112	from dataclasses import dataclass
1113	
1114	import httpx
1115	
1116	TOKEN_URL = "https://accounts.spotify.com/api/token"
1117	API_BASE = "https://api.spotify.com/v1"
1118	
1119	
1120	class SpotifyError(Exception):
1121	    """Base exception for Spotify API failures."""
1122	
1123	
1124	class InvalidGrantError(SpotifyError):
1125	    """The refresh_token is no longer valid; human re-authorization needed."""
1126	
1127	
1128	@dataclass(frozen=True)
1129	class TokenResult:
1130	    access_token: str
1131	    refresh_token: str
1132	
1133	
1134	class SpotifyClient:
1135	    """Thin HTTP client for Spotify. Stateless."""
1136	
1137	    def __init__(
1138	        self,
1139	        client_id: str,
1140	        client_secret: str,
1141	        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,
1142	    ) -> None:
1143	        self._client_id = client_id
1144	        self._client_secret = client_secret
1145	        self._http = http_client or httpx.Client(timeout=30.0)
1146	
1147	    def _basic_auth(self) -> str:
1148	        raw = f"{self._client_id}:{self._client_secret}".encode()
1149	        return "Basic " + base64.b64encode(raw).decode()
1150	
1151	    def refresh_access_token(self, refresh_token: str) -> TokenResult:
1152	        resp = self._http.post(
1153	            TOKEN_URL,
1154	            headers={
1155	                "Authorization": self._basic_auth(),
1156	                "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
1157	            },
1158	            data={"grant_type": "refresh_token", "refresh_token": refresh_token},
1159	        )
1160	        if resp.status_code == 400:
1161	            body = resp.json()
1162	            if body.get("error") == "invalid_grant":
1163	                raise InvalidGrantError(body.get("error_description", "invalid_grant"))
1164	        if resp.status_code >= 400:
1165	            raise SpotifyError(f"token endpoint {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}")
1166	
1167	        body = resp.json()
1168	        return TokenResult(
1169	            access_token=body["access_token"],
1170	            refresh_token=body.get("refresh_token", refresh_token),
1171	        )
1172	```
1173

1178	```

1253	    def recently_played(
1254	        self,
1255	        access_token: str,
1256	        after_ms: int,
1257	        limit: int = 50,
1258	    ) -> list[dict]:
1259	        resp = self._http.get(
1260	            f"{API_BASE}/me/player/recently-played",
1261	            params={"after": after_ms, "limit": limit},
1262	            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}"},
1263	        )
1264	        if resp.status_code == 401:
1265	            raise SpotifyError(
1266	                "spotify returned 401; access_token likely expired mid-flight"
1267	            )
1268	        if resp.status_code == 403:
1269	            raise SpotifyError(f"spotify forbidden: {resp.text}")
1270	        if resp.status_code >= 400:
1271	            raise SpotifyError(f"recently-played {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}")
1272	
1273	        items = resp.json().get("items", [])
1274	        return items

1504	- [ ] **Step 3: Create `state.py`**
1505	
1506	```python
1507	from __future__ import annotations
1508	
1509	import httpx
1510	
1511	
1512	class StateStore:
1513	    """KV access via the worker outbound handler at kv.internal."""
1514	
1515	    def __init__(
1516	        self,
1517	        base_url: str,
1518	        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,
1519	    ) -> None:
1520	        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
1521	        self._http = http_client or httpx.Client(timeout=10.0)
1522	
1523	    def get(self, key: str) -> str | None:
1524	        resp = self._http.get(f"{self._base_url}/{key}")
1525	        if resp.status_code == 404:
1526	            return None
1527	        if resp.status_code != 200:
1528	            raise RuntimeError(f"state.get({key}) status={resp.status_code}")
1529	        return resp.text
1530	
1531	    def put(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
1532	        resp = self._http.put(f"{self._base_url}/{key}", content=value)
1533	        if resp.status_code not in (200, 204):
1534	            raise RuntimeError(f"state.put({key}) status={resp.status_code}")
1535	```

1652	```python
1653	from __future__ import annotations
1654	
1655	import json
1656	from datetime import datetime
1657	
1658	import pyarrow as pa
1659	
1660	
1661	def _parse_iso_utc(ts: str) -> datetime:
1662	    # Spotify returns 2026-04-20T10:00:00.000Z; replace Z for fromisoformat
1663	    if ts.endswith("Z"):
1664	        ts = ts[:-1] + "+00:00"
1665	    return datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
1666	
1667	
1668	def _build_artist(artist: dict) -> dict:
1669	    return {
1670	        "id": artist["id"],
1671	        "name": artist["name"],
1672	        "uri": artist.get("uri"),
1673	    }
1674	
1675	
1676	def _build_album(album: dict | None) -> dict | None:
1677	    if album is None:
1678	        return None
1679	    return {
1680	        "id": album["id"],
1681	        "name": album["name"],
1682	        "uri": album.get("uri"),
1683	        "album_type": album.get("album_type"),
1684	        "release_date": album.get("release_date"),
1685	        "total_tracks": album.get("total_tracks"),
1686	    }
1687	
1688	
1689	def _build_track(track: dict) -> dict:
1690	    return {
1691	        "id": track["id"],
1692	        "name": track["name"],
1693	        "uri": track.get("uri"),
1694	        "duration_ms": track.get("duration_ms"),
1695	        "explicit": track.get("explicit"),
1696	        "popularity": track.get("popularity"),
1697	        "isrc": (track.get("external_ids") or {}).get("isrc"),
1698	        "album": _build_album(track.get("album")),
1699	        "artists": [_build_artist(a) for a in track.get("artists", [])],
1700	    }
1701	
1702	
1703	def _build_context(context: dict | None) -> dict | None:
1704	    if context is None:
1705	        return None
1706	    return {"type": context.get("type"), "uri": context.get("uri")}
1707	
1708	
1709	_ARTIST_TYPE = pa.struct(
1710	    [
1711	        ("id", pa.string()),
1712	        ("name", pa.string()),
1713	        ("uri", pa.string()),
1714	    ]
1715	)
1716	
1717	_ALBUM_TYPE = pa.struct(
1718	    [
1719	        ("id", pa.string()),
1720	        ("name", pa.string()),
1721	        ("uri", pa.string()),
1722	        ("album_type", pa.string()),
1723	        ("release_date", pa.string()),
1724	        ("total_tracks", pa.int32()),
1725	    ]
1726	)
1727	
1728	_TRACK_TYPE = pa.struct(
1729	    [
1730	        ("id", pa.string()),
1731	        ("name", pa.string()),
1732	        ("uri", pa.string()),
1733	        ("duration_ms", pa.int32()),
1734	        ("explicit", pa.bool_()),
1735	        ("popularity", pa.int32()),
1736	        ("isrc", pa.string()),
1737	        ("album", _ALBUM_TYPE),
1738	        ("artists", pa.list_(_ARTIST_TYPE)),
1739	    ]
1740	)
1741	
1742	_CONTEXT_TYPE = pa.struct(
1743	    [
1744	        ("type", pa.string()),
1745	        ("uri", pa.string()),
1746	    ]
1747	)
1748	
1749	_ARROW_SCHEMA = pa.schema(
1750	    [
1751	        ("played_at", pa.timestamp("us", tz="UTC")),
1752	        ("played_at_ms", pa.int64()),
1753	        ("track", _TRACK_TYPE),
1754	        ("context", _CONTEXT_TYPE),
1755	        ("_raw_json", pa.string()),
1756	        ("_ingested_at", pa.timestamp("us", tz="UTC")),
1757	    ]
1758	)
1759	
1760	
1761	def build_arrow_table(items: list[dict], ingested_at: datetime) -> pa.Table:
1762	    """Convert Spotify recently-played items into a pyarrow.Table matching schema.py."""
1763	    rows = []
1764	    for item in items:
1765	        played_at = _parse_iso_utc(item["played_at"])
1766	        rows.append(
1767	            {
1768	                "played_at": played_at,
1769	                "played_at_ms": int(played_at.timestamp() * 1000),
1770	                "track": _build_track(item["track"]),
1771	                "context": _build_context(item.get("context")),
1772	                "_raw_json": json.dumps(item, separators=(",", ":")),
1773	                "_ingested_at": ingested_at,
1774	            }
1775	        )
1776	    return pa.Table.from_pylist(rows, schema=_ARROW_SCHEMA)
1777	```

{
  "issueId": "TA-469",
  "body": "# 実装計画 (2/4): Phase C + D + E\n\n## Phase C — PyIceberg schema\n\n### Task C1: Schema module with types, partition spec, sort order\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/schema.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create schema module**\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\nIceberg schema for the spotify.recently_played table.\n\nThe schema mirrors the Spotify API item structure (fully nested) with two\naudit columns appended. Transformation/flatten is deferred to dbt.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pyiceberg.partitioning import PartitionField, PartitionSpec\nfrom pyiceberg.schema import Schema\nfrom pyiceberg.table.sorting import SortField, SortOrder\nfrom pyiceberg.transforms import DayTransform, IdentityTransform\nfrom pyiceberg.types import (\n    BooleanType,\n    IntegerType,\n    ListType,\n    LongType,\n    NestedField,\n    StringType,\n    StructType,\n    TimestamptzType,\n)\n\nARTIST_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(101, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(102, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(103, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(201, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(202, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(203, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(204, \"album_type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(205, \"release_date\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(206, \"total_tracks\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n)\n\nTRACK_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(301, \"id\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(302, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(303, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(304, \"duration_ms\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(305, \"explicit\", BooleanType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(306, \"popularity\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(307, \"isrc\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(308, \"album\", ALBUM_STRUCT, required=False),\n    NestedField(\n        field_id=309,\n        name=\"artists\",\n        field_type=ListType(\n            element_id=310,\n            element_type=ARTIST_STRUCT,\n            element_required=True,\n        ),\n        required=True,\n    ),\n)\n\nCONTEXT_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(401, \"type\", StringType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(402, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nRECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA = Schema(\n    NestedField(1, \"played_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(2, \"played_at_ms\", LongType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(3, \"track\", TRACK_STRUCT, required=True),\n    NestedField(4, \"context\", CONTEXT_STRUCT, required=False),\n    NestedField(5, \"_raw_json\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(6, \"_ingested_at\", TimestamptzType(), required=True),\n    identifier_field_ids=[1, 301],\n)\n\nPARTITION_SPEC = PartitionSpec(\n    PartitionField(\n        source_id=1,\n        field_id=1000,\n        transform=DayTransform(),\n        name=\"played_at_day\",\n    ),\n)\n\nSORT_ORDER = SortOrder(\n    SortField(source_id=1, transform=IdentityTransform()),\n)\n\nTABLE_PROPERTIES = {\n    \"write.format.default\": \"parquet\",\n    \"write.parquet.compression-codec\": \"zstd\",\n    \"format-version\": \"2\",\n}\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/schema.py\ngit add retry helpers at module level**\n\n```python\nimport time\n\nMAX_RETRIES = 3\nBACKOFF_BASE_SEC = 0.5\n\n\ndef _should_retry(status_code: int) -> bool:\n    return status_code == 429 or 500 <= status_code < 600\n\n\ndef _retry_after_sec(response: httpx.Response) -> float:\n    header = response.headers.get(\"Retry-After\")\n    if header is not None:\n        try:\n            return self,\n        access_token: str,\n        after_ms: int,\n        limit: int = 50,\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        url = f\"{API_BASE}/me/player/recently-played\"\n        params = {\"after\": after_ms, \"limit\": limit}\n        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {access_token}\"}\n\n        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES + 1):\n            resp = self._http.get(url, params=params, headers=headers)\n            if not _should_retry(resp.status_code):\n                break\n            if attempt == MAX_RETRIES:\n                raise SpotifyError(\n                    f\"recently-played failed after {MAX_RETRIES + 1} attempts: \"\n                    f\"status={resp.status_code}\"\n                )\n            sleep_sec = _retry_after_sec(resp) or BACKOFF_BASE_SEC * (2**attempt)\n            time.sleep(sleep_sec)\n\n        if resp.status_code == 401:\n            raise SpotifyError(\"spotify returned 401; access_token likely expired mid-flight\")\n        if resp.status_code == 403:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"spotify forbidden: {resp.text}\")\n        if resp.status_code >= 400:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"recently-played {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\")\n        return resp.json().get(\"items\", [])\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — expect PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5:    def __init__(\n        self,\n        base_url: str,\n        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n        self._http = NestedField(102, \"name\", StringType(), required=True),\n    NestedField(103, \"uri\", StringType(), required=False),\n)\n\nALBUM_STRUCT = StructType(\n    NestedField(201, \"id\", StringType(), \"duration_ms\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(305, \"explicit\", BooleanType(), required=False),\n    NestedField(306, \"popularity\", IntegerType(), required=False),\n add retry helpers at module level**\n\n```python\nimport time\n\nMAX_RETRIES = 3\nBACKOFF_BASE_SEC = 0.5\n\n\ndef _should_retry(status_code: int) -> bool:\n    return status_code == 429 or 500 <= status_code < 600\n\n\ndef _retry_after_sec(response: httpx.Response) -> float:\n    header = response.headers.get(\"Retry-After\")\n    if header is not None:\n        try:\n            return min(float(header), 30.0)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n    return 0.0\n```\n\nAnd replace the `recently_played` method body with:\n\n```python\n    def recently_played(\n        self,\n        access_token: str,\n        after_ms: int,\n        limit: int = 50,\n    ) -> list[dict]:\n        url = f\"{API_BASE}/me/player/recently-played\"\n        params = {\"after\": after_ms, \"limit\": limit}\n        headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {access_token}\"}\n\n        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES + 1):\n            resp = self._http.get(url, params=params, headers=headers)\n            if not _should_retry(resp.status_code):\n                break\n            if attempt == MAX_RETRIES:\n                raise SpotifyError(\n                    f\"recently-played failed after {MAX_RETRIES + 1} attempts: \"\n                    f\"status={resp.status_code}\"\n                )\n            sleep_sec = _retry_after_sec(resp) or BACKOFF_BASE_SEC * (2**attempt)\n            time.sleep(sleep_sec)\n\n        if resp.status_code == 401:\n            raise SpotifyError(\"spotify returned 401; access_token likely expired mid-flight\")\n        if resp.status_code == 403:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"spotify forbidden: {resp.text}\")\n        if resp.status_code >= 400:\n            raise SpotifyError(f\"recently-played {resp.status_code}: {resp.text}\")\n        return resp.json().get(\"items\", [])\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — expect PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: [ ] **Step 3: Create `state.py`**\n\n```python\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport httpx\n\n\nclass StateStore:\n    \"\"\"KV access via the worker outbound handler at kv.internal.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        base_url: str,\n        http_client: httpx.Client | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._base_url = base_url.rstrip(\"/\")\n        self._http = http_client or httpx.Client(timeout=10.0)\n\n    def get(self, key: str) -> str | None:\n        resp = self._http.get(f\"{self._base_url}/{key}\")\n        if resp.status_code == 404:\n            return None\n        if resp.status_code != 200:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"state.get({key}) status={resp.status_code}\")\n        return resp.text\n\n    def put(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:\n        resp = self._http.put(f\"{self._base_url}/{key}\", content=value)\n        if resp.status_code not in (200, 204):\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"state.put({key}) status={resp.status_code}\")\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — expect PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container/src/spotify_iceberg/state.py 1851	```python
1852	from pyiceberg.catalog import Catalog
1853	from pyiceberg.table import Table
1854	
1855	from .schema import PARTITION_SPEC, RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA, SORT_ORDER, TABLE_PROPERTIES
1856	
1857	
1858	def ensure_table(catalog: Catalog, identifier: tuple[str, str]) -> Table:
1859	    namespace, _ = identifier
1860	    if (namespace,) not in catalog.list_namespaces():
1861	        catalog.create_namespace(namespace)
1862	    try:
1863	        return catalog.load_table(identifier)
1864	    except Exception:
1865	        return catalog.create_table(
1866	            identifier=identifier,
1867	            schema=RECENTLY_PLAYED_SCHEMA,
1868	            partition_spec=PARTITION_SPEC,
1869	            sort_order=SORT_ORDER,
1870	            properties=TABLE_PROPERTIES,
1871	        )
1872	```

1938	from dataclasses import dataclass
1939	
1940	
1941	@dataclass(frozen=True)
1942	class AppendResult:
1943	    rows_written: int
1944	    snapshot_id: int | None
1945	
1946	
1947	def append_items(
1948	    catalog: Catalog,
1949	    identifier: tuple[str, str],
1950	    items: list[dict],
1951	    ingested_at: datetime,
1952	) -> AppendResult:
1953	    if not items:
1954	        return AppendResult(rows_written=0, snapshot_id=None)
1955	    table = ensure_table(catalog, identifier)
1956	    arrow_table = build_arrow_table(items, ingested_at=ingested_at)
1957	    table.append(arrow_table)
1958	    current = table.current_snapshot()
1959	    return AppendResult(
1960	        rows_written=len(items),
1961	        snapshot_id=current.snapshot_id if current else None,
1962	    )
1963	```

2057	from __future__ import annotations
2058	
2059	from dataclasses import dataclass
2060	from datetime import datetime, timezone
2061	from typing import Callable
2062	
2063	from pyiceberg.catalog import Catalog
2064	
2065	from .iceberg_writer import AppendResult, append_items
2066	from .spotify import SpotifyClient
2067	from .state import StateStore
2068	
2069	TABLE_IDENTIFIER: tuple[str, str] = ("spotify", "recently_played")
2070	
2071	
2072	@dataclass(frozen=True)
2073	class PipelineResult:
2074	    rows_written: int
2075	    new_cursor_ms: int | None
2076	    snapshot_id: int | None
2077	
2078	
2079	def run_recently_played(
2080	    *,
2081	    state: StateStore,
2082	    spotify: SpotifyClient,
2083	    catalog: Catalog,
2084	    now: Callable[[], datetime] = lambda: datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),
2085	    first_run_lookback_ms: int = 60 * 60 * 1000,
2086	) -> PipelineResult:
2087	    refresh_token = state.get("refresh_token")
2088	    if refresh_token is None:
2089	        raise RuntimeError(
2090	            "refresh_token not found in KV — run scripts/get_refresh_token.py "
2091	            "and load the value with `wrangler kv key put`"
2092	        )
2093	
2094	    token = spotify.refresh_access_token(refresh_token)
2095	    if token.refresh_token != refresh_token:
2096	        state.put("refresh_token", token.refresh_token)
2097	
2098	    cursor_str = state.get("cursor")
2099	    if cursor_str is None:
2100	        after_ms = int(now().timestamp() * 1000) - first_run_lookback_ms
2101	    else:
2102	        after_ms = int(cursor_str)
2103	
2104	    items = spotify.recently_played(token.access_token, after_ms=after_ms)
2105	    if not items:
2106	        return PipelineResult(rows_written=0, new_cursor_ms=None, snapshot_id=None)
2107	
2108	    append_result: AppendResult = append_items(
2109	        catalog,
2110	        TABLE_IDENTIFIER,
2111	        items,
2112	        ingested_at=now(),
2113	    )
2114	
2115	    latest_ms = max(_played_at_ms(item) for item in items)
2116	    state.put("cursor", str(latest_ms))
2117	
2118	    return PipelineResult(
2119	        rows_written=append_result.rows_written,
2120	        new_cursor_ms=latest_ms,
2121	        snapshot_id=append_result.snapshot_id,
2122	    )
2123	
2124	
2125	def _played_at_ms(item: dict) -> int:
2126	    ts = item["played_at"]
2127	    if ts.endswith("Z"):
2128	        ts = ts[:-1] + "+00:00"
2129	    return int(datetime.fromisoformat(ts).timestamp() * 1000)
2130	```

tuple[str, str],\n    items: list[dict],\n    ingested_at: datetime,\n) -> AppendResult:\n    if not items:\n        return AppendResult(rows_written=0, snapshot_id=None)\n    table = ensure_table(catalog, identifier)\n    arrow_table = build_arrow_table(items, ingested_at=ingested_at)\n    table.append(arrow_table)\n    current = table.current_snapshot()\n    return 2313	from __future__ import annotations

2318	from fastapi import FastAPI

2330	app = FastAPI(title="spotify-iceberg")

2394	## Phase I — Deployment prep
2395	
2396	### Task I1: Container README with setup instructions
2397	
2398	**Files:**
2399	- Create: `spotify-iceberg/README.md`
2400	
2401	- [ ] **Step 1: Write README**
2402	
2403	```markdown
2404	# spotify-iceberg
2405	
2406	Cloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that appends Spotify recently-played
2407	history to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog every hour.
2408	
2409	Spec: [Linear TA-469](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469/spotify-r2-iceberg-データパイプライン設計)
2410	
2411	## Setup (one-time)
2412	
2413	### 1. Enable R2 Data Catalog
2414	
2415	```bash
2416	wrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake
2417	```
2418	
2419	Note the printed **Catalog URI** and **Warehouse** — you'll need them in step 6.
2420	
2421	### 2. Create Spotify Developer app
2422	
2423	At <https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard>, create an app and register
2424	`http://localhost:8888/callback` as a Redirect URI. Note the Client ID and
2425	Client Secret.
2426	
2427	### 3. Obtain refresh_token locally
2428	
2429	```bash
2430	uv run --with spotipy python scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py
2431	```
2432	
2433	Follow the browser flow; the script prints the refresh_token.
2434	
2435	### 4. Create KV namespace
2436	
2437	```bash
2438	cd spotify-iceberg/worker
2439	wrangler kv namespace create SPOTIFY_STATE_KV
2440	```
2441	
2442	Copy the printed `id` into `wrangler.jsonc` under `kv_namespaces[0].id`.
2443	
2444	### 5. Store refresh_token in KV
2445	
2446	```bash
2447	wrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token "<value-from-step-3>"
2448	```
2449	
2450	### 6. Register secrets (from spotify-iceberg/worker/)
2451	
2452	```bash
2453	wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID
2454	wrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET
2455	wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_URI
2456	wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_TOKEN
2457	wrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE
2458	wrangler secret put R2_ENDPOINT
2459	wrangler secret put R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID
2460	wrangler secret put R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY
2461	```
2462	
2463	### 7. Deploy
2464	
2465	```bash
2466	cd spotify-iceberg/worker && pnpm deploy
2467	```
2468	
2469	### 8. Verify
2470	
2471	- Trigger manually: `curl -X POST https://spotify-iceberg.<account>.workers.dev/trigger`
2472	- Tail logs: `wrangler tail`
2473	- Query via DuckDB:
2474	
2475	```sql
2476	INSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;
2477	ATTACH '<R2_CATALOG_URI>' AS r2 (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT_TYPE S3_TABLES);
2478	SELECT played_at, track.name, track.artists[1].name
2479	FROM r2.spotify.recently_played
2480	ORDER BY played_at DESC LIMIT 10;
2481	```
2482	
2483	## Development
2484	
2485	- Worker: `cd worker && pnpm test:run && pnpm typecheck && pnpm check`
2486	- Container: `cd container && uv run pytest && uv run ruff check .`
2487	
2488	## Open TODOs
2489	
2490	See [TA-469 Open Questions](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469).
2491	```
2492

2498	```

2567	name: spotify-iceberg CI

2663	- **All non-interactive tasks follow TDD**: failing test → implement → passing test → commit
2664	- **No network in tests**: `respx` mocks Spotify API + KV outbound; `SqlCatalog` + local filesystem replaces R2
2665	- **Phase 2 (separate plan)**: `top_tracks_daily`, `saved_tracks`, `audio_features` enrichment, Streaming History bulk backfill, Workflows migration — all listed as Open Questions in TA-469
2666

test_run_returns_pipeline_result() -> None:\n    with patch(\"spotify_iceberg.main.run_recently_played\") as mock_run:\n        mock_run.return_value = PipelineResult(\n            rows_written=3,\n = resp.json()\n    assert body[\"rows_written\"] == 3\n    assert body[\"new_cursor_ms\"] == 1713500400000\n    assert body[\"snapshot_id\"] == 4242\n\n\ndef test_run_surfaces_errors_as_500() -> None:\n    with patch(\"spotify_iceberg.main.run_recently_played\", side_effect=RuntimeError(\"boom\")):\n        client = TestClient(app)\n        resp = client.post(\"/run\")\n    assert resp.status_code == 500\n    assert \"boom\" in resp.json()[\"error\"]\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run** — expect FAIL\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Replace `main.py`**\n\n```python\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom dataclasses import asdict\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nfrom pyiceberg.catalog.rest import RestCatalog\n\nfrom .config import Settings\nfrom .pipeline import run_recently_played\nfrom .spotify import SpotifyClient\nfrom .state import StateStore\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"spotify_iceberg\")\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(message)s\")\n\napp = FastAPI(title=\"spotify-iceberg\")\n\n\ndef _build_catalog(settings: Settings) -> RestCatalog:\n    return RestCatalog(\n        \"r2\",\n        **{\n            \"uri\": settings.r2_catalog_uri,\n            \"token\": settings.r2_catalog_token,\n            \"warehouse\": settings.r2_catalog_warehouse,\n            \"s3.endpoint\": settings.r2_endpoint,\n            \"s3.access-key-id\": settings.r2_access_key_id,\n            \"s3.secret-access-key\": settings.r2_secret_access_key,\n            \"s3.region\": \"auto\",\n        },\n    )\n\n\n@app.get(\"/health\")\ndef health() -> dict[str, str]:\n    return {\"status\": \"ok\"}\n\n\n@app.post(\"/run\")\ndef run() -> JSONResponse:\n    settings = Settings()\n    state = StateStore(base_url=settings.kv_base_url)\n    spotify = SpotifyClient(\n        client_id=settings.spotify_client_id,\n        client_secret=settings.spotify_client_secret,\n    )\n    catalog = _build_catalog(settings)\n\n    try:\n        result = run_recently_played(\n            state=state,\n            spotify=spotify,\n            catalog=catalog,\n            first_run_lookback_ms=settings.first_run_lookback_ms,\n        )\n    except Exception as exc:\n        logger.exception(\"pipeline failed\")\n        return JSONResponse({\"error\": str(exc)}, status_code=500)\n\n    logger.info(\"pipeline ok rows=%s cursor=%s\", result.rows_written, result.new_cursor_ms)\n    return JSONResponse(asdict(result))\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — expect PASS\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/container\ngit commit -m \"feat(spotify-iceberg): FastAPI /run endpoint wires pipeline\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase I — Deployment prep\n\n### Task I1: README with setup instructions\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `spotify-iceberg/README.md`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write README**\n\n````markdown\n# spotify-iceberg\n\nCloudflare Worker + Container pipeline that appends Spotify recently-played\nhistory to an Apache Iceberg table on R2 Data Catalog every hour.\n\nSpec: [Linear TA-469](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469)\n\n## Setup (one-time)\n\n### 1. Enable R2 Data Catalog\n```bash\nwrangler r2 bucket catalog enable data-lake\n```\nNote the printed **Catalog URI** and **Warehouse**.\n\n### 2. Create Spotify Developer app\nAt <https://developer.spotify.com/dashboard>, register `http://localhost:8888/callback` as a Redirect URI.\n\n### 3. Obtain refresh_token locally\n```bash\nuv run --with spotipy python scripts/spotify/get_refresh_token.py\n```\n\n### 4. Create KV namespace\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker\nwrangler kv namespace create SPOTIFY_STATE_KV\n```\nCopy the printed `id` into `wrangler.jsonc`.\n\n### 5. Store refresh_token in KV\n```bash\nwrangler kv key put --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV refresh_token \"<value-from-step-3>\"\n```\n\n### 6. Register secrets (from `spotify-iceberg/worker/`)\n```bash\nwrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID\nwrangler secret put SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_URI\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_TOKEN\nwrangler secret put R2_CATALOG_WAREHOUSE\nwrangler secret put R2_ENDPOINT\nwrangler secret put R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID\nwrangler secret put R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\n```\n\n### 7. Deploy\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker && pnpm deploy\n```\n\n### 8. Verify\n- Trigger manually: `curl -X POST https://spotify-iceberg.<account>.workers.dev/trigger`\n- Tail logs: `wrangler tail`\n- Query via DuckDB:\n```sql\nINSTALL iceberg; LOAD iceberg;\nATTACH '<R2_CATALOG_URI>' AS r2 (TYPE ICEBERG, ENDPOINT_TYPE S3_TABLES);\nSELECT played_at, track.name, track.artists[1].name\nFROM r2.spotify.recently_played\nORDER BY played_at DESC LIMIT 10;\n```\n\n## Development\n\n- Worker: `cd worker && pnpm test:run && pnpm typecheck && pnpm check`\n- Container: `cd container && uv run pytest && uv run ruff check .`\n\n## Open TODOs\n\nSee [TA-469 Open Questions](https://linear.app/ta93abe/issue/TA-469).\n````\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add spotify-iceberg/README.md\ngit commit -m \"docs(spotify-iceberg): setup and verification instructions\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task I2: Wrangler local dev smoke (manual)\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Run local dev**\n\n```bash\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker && pnpm dev\n```\n\nExpected: wrangler starts, container image builds, `http://localhost:8787/` returns `spotify-iceberg worker`. Stop with `Ctrl+C`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Deploy**\n\n```bash\npnpm deploy\n```\n\nExpected: Worker + container deploy without errors.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: No commit**\n\n---\n\n### Task I3: E2E verification (manual, after setup 1–6)\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Manually trigger**\n\n```bash\ncurl -X POST https://spotify-iceberg.<acct>.workers.dev/trigger\n```\n\nExpected: `{\"rows_written\": N, \"new_cursor_ms\": ..., \"snapshot_id\": ...}`.\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Verify KV cursor advanced**\n\n```bash\nwrangler kv key get --binding=SPOTIFY_STATE_KV cursor\n```\n\nExpected: epoch-ms integer.\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: DuckDB query** (use the query from README §8) — expect ≥ N rows.\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Wait for next cron (up to 1 hour)**\n\nExpected: `wrangler tail` shows `container /run status=200`; row count grows if new plays happened.\n\n---\n\n## Phase J — CI integration\n\n### Task J1: GitHub Actions workflow\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `.github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Create workflow**\n\n```yaml\nname: spotify-iceberg CI\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    paths:\n      - \"spotify-iceberg/**\"\n      - \".github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\"\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - \"spotify-iceberg/**\"\n      - \".github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\"\n\njobs:\n  worker:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    defaults:\n      run:\n        working-directory: spotify-iceberg/worker\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4\n        with:\n          version: 9\n      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4\n        with:\n          node-version: 20\n          cache: pnpm\n          cache-dependency-path: spotify-iceberg/worker/pnpm-lock.yaml\n      - run: pnpm install --frozen-lockfile\n      - run: pnpm check\n      - run: pnpm typecheck\n      - run: pnpm test:run\n\n  container:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    defaults:\n      run:\n        working-directory: spotify-iceberg/container\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v4\n        with:\n          version: latest\n      - run: uv sync --extra dev\n      - run: uv run ruff check .\n      - run: uv run pytest -v\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add .github/workflows/spotify-iceberg.yml\ngit commit -m \"ci(spotify-iceberg): add worker+container CI workflow\"\n```\n\n---\n\n## Phase K — Final polish\n\n### Task K1: Link plan and PR to Linear + update status\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: After first PR opens, post a comment on TA-469 linking to it** (manual or via Linear MCP)\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Move TA-469 status**\n  - \"In Progress\" when Task A1 begins\n  - \"In Review\" when PR opens\n  - \"Done\" when merged\n\nNo code — Linear status transitions only.\n\n---\n\n## Full test matrix\n\nRun everything before declaring Phase 1 complete:\n\n```bash\n# Worker\ncd spotify-iceberg/worker\npnpm check\npnpm typecheck\npnpm test:run\n\n# Container\ncd ../container\nuv run ruff check .\nuv run pytest -v\n```\n\nExpected: all green.\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\n- **Phases A–K** (~24 tasks) producing a deployable hourly pipeline\n- **All non-interactive tasks follow TDD**: failing test → implement → passing test → commit\n- **No network in tests**: `respx` mocks Spotify API + KV outbound; `SqlCatalog` + local filesystem replaces R2\n- **Phase 2 (separate issue)**: `top_tracks_daily`, `saved_tracks`, `audio_features` enrichment, Streaming History bulk backfill, Workflows migration — all listed as Open Questions in the issue description\n\n---\n\n## 実行方式\n\n1. **Subagent-Driven（推奨）** — タスクごとに fresh subagent を起動、二段階レビュー\n2. **Inline Execution** — このセッションでそのまま実行、checkpoint 単位でレビュー\n\n実装開始時に選択。"
}

> AGENT

worktree 配置の preference がないので確認させてください。

## worktree の配置場所

| | 配置先 | 特徴 |
|---|---|---|
| **A** | `.worktrees/` (project-local, hidden) | リポジトリ内に隠しディレクトリ。プロジェクト固有 |
| **B** | `~/.config/superpowers/worktrees/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/` (global) | ホームディレクトリ下。プロジェクト外 |

推奨は **A** — プロジェクト内で完結し、他の worker 実装（`d1-to-iceberg/`, `dlt-github/` 等）の並びに自然にフィットします。.gitignore 追加が必要ですが1行で済みます。

**A / B どちらにしますか？**

> DEVELOPER

一旦コミットしてほしい

1	# Implementer Subagent Prompt Template
2	
3	Use this template when dispatching an implementer subagent.
4	
5	```
6	Task tool (general-purpose):
7	  description: "Implement Task N: [task name]"
8	  prompt: |
9	    You are implementing Task N: [task name]
10	
11	    ## Task Description
12	
13	    [FULL TEXT of task from plan - paste it here, don't make subagent read file]
14	
15	    ## Context
16	
17	    [Scene-setting: where this fits, dependencies, architectural context]
18	
19	    ## Before You Begin
20	
21	    If you have questions about:
22	    - The requirements or acceptance criteria
23	    - The approach or implementation strategy
24	    - Dependencies or assumptions
25	    - Anything unclear in the task description
26	
27	    **Ask them now.** Raise any concerns before starting work.
28	
29	    ## Your Job
30	
31	    Once you're clear on requirements:
32	    1. Implement exactly what the task specifies
33	    2. Write tests (following TDD if task says to)
34	    3. Verify implementation works
35	    4. Commit your work
36	    5. Self-review (see below)
37	    6. Report back
38	
39	    Work from: [directory]
40	
41	    **While you work:** If you encounter something unexpected or unclear, **ask questions**.
42	    It's always OK to pause and clarify. Don't guess or make assumptions.
43	
44	    ## Code Organization
45	
46	    You reason best about code you can hold in context at once, and your edits are more
47	    reliable when files are focused. Keep this in mind:
48	    - Follow the file structure defined in the plan
49	    - Each file should have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface
50	    - If a file you're creating is growing beyond the plan's intent, stop and report
51	      it as DONE_WITH_CONCERNS — don't split files on your own without plan guidance
52	    - If an existing file you're modifying is already large or tangled, work carefully
53	      and note it as a concern in your report
54	    - In existing codebases, follow established patterns. Improve code you're touching
55	      the way a good developer would, but don't restructure things outside your task.
56	
57	    ## When You're in Over Your Head
58	
59	    It is always OK to stop and say "this is too hard for me." Bad work is worse than
60	    no work. You will not be penalized for escalating.
61	
62	    **STOP and escalate when:**
63	    - The task requires architectural decisions with multiple valid approaches
64	    - You need to understand code beyond what was provided and can't find clarity
65	    - You feel uncertain about whether your approach is correct
66	    - The task involves restructuring existing code in ways the plan didn't anticipate
67	    - You've been reading file after file trying to understand the system without progress
68	
69	    **How to escalate:** Report back with status BLOCKED or NEEDS_CONTEXT. Describe
70	    specifically what you're stuck on, what you've tried, and what kind of help you need.
71	    The controller can provide more context, re-dispatch with a more capable model,
72	    or break the task into smaller pieces.
73	
74	    ## Before Reporting Back: Self-Review
75	
76	    Review your work with fresh eyes. Ask yourself:
77	
78	    **Completeness:**
79	    - Did I fully implement everything in the spec?
80	    - Did I miss any requirements?
81	    - Are there edge cases I didn't handle?
82	
83	    **Quality:**
84	    - Is this my best work?
85	    - Are names clear and accurate (match what things do, not how they work)?
86	    - Is the code clean and maintainable?
87	
88	    **Discipline:**
89	    - Did I avoid overbuilding (YAGNI)?
90	    - Did I only build what was requested?
91	    - Did I follow existing patterns in the codebase?
92	
93	    **Testing:**
94	    - Do tests actually verify behavior (not just mock behavior)?
95	    - Did I follow TDD if required?
96	    - Are tests comprehensive?
97	
98	    If you find issues during self-review, fix them now before reporting.
99	
100	    ## Report Format
101	
102	    When done, report:
103	    - **Status:** DONE | DONE_WITH_CONCERNS | BLOCKED | NEEDS_CONTEXT
104	    - What you implemented (or what you attempted, if blocked)
105	    - What you tested and test results
106	    - Files changed
107	    - Self-review findings (if any)
108	    - Any issues or concerns
109	
110	    Use DONE_WITH_CONCERNS if you completed the work but have doubts about correctness.
111	    Use BLOCKED if you cannot complete the task. Use NEEDS_CONTEXT if you need
112	    information that wasn't provided. Never silently produce work you're unsure about.
113	```
114

1	# Code Quality Reviewer Prompt Template
2	
3	Use this template when dispatching a code quality reviewer subagent.
4	
5	**Purpose:** Verify implementation is well-built (clean, tested, maintainable)
6	
7	**Only dispatch after spec compliance review passes.**
8	
9	```
10	Task tool (superpowers:code-reviewer):
11	  Use template at requesting-code-review/code-reviewer.md
12	
13	  WHAT_WAS_IMPLEMENTED: [from implementer's report]
14	  PLAN_OR_REQUIREMENTS: Task N from [plan-file]
15	  BASE_SHA: [commit before task]
16	  HEAD_SHA: [current commit]
17	  DESCRIPTION: [task summary]
18	```
19	
20	**In addition to standard code quality concerns, the reviewer should check:**
21	- Does each file have one clear responsibility with a well-defined interface?
22	- Are units decomposed so they can be understood and tested independently?
23	- Is the implementation following the file structure from the plan?
24	- Did this implementation create new files that are already large, or significantly grow existing files? (Don't flag pre-existing file sizes — focus on what this change contributed.)
25	
26	**Code reviewer returns:** Strengths, Issues (Critical/Important/Minor), Assessment
27	
```

1	# Spec Compliance Reviewer Prompt Template
2	
3	Use this template when dispatching a spec compliance reviewer subagent.
4	
5	**Purpose:** Verify implementer built what was requested (nothing more, nothing less)
6	
7	```
8	Task tool (general-purpose):
9	  description: "Review spec compliance for Task N"
10	  prompt: |
11	    You are reviewing whether an implementation matches its specification.
12	
13	    ## What Was Requested
14	
15	    [FULL TEXT of task requirements]
16	
17	    ## What Implementer Claims They Built
18	
19	    [From implementer's report]
20	
21	    ## CRITICAL: Do Not Trust the Report
22	
23	    The implementer finished suspiciously quickly. Their report may be incomplete,
24	    inaccurate, or optimistic. You MUST verify everything independently.
25	
26	    **DO NOT:**
27	    - Take their word for what they implemented
28	    - Trust their claims about completeness
29	    - Accept their interpretation of requirements
30	
31	    **DO:**
32	    - Read the actual code they wrote
33	    - Compare actual implementation to requirements line by line
34	    - Check for missing pieces they claimed to implement
35	    - Look for extra features they didn't mention
36	
37	    ## Your Job
38	
39	    Read the implementation code and verify:
40	
41	    **Missing requirements:**
42	    - Did they implement everything that was requested?
43	    - Are there requirements they skipped or missed?
44	    - Did they claim something works but didn't actually implement it?
45	
46	    **Extra/unneeded work:**
47	    - Did they build things that weren't requested?
48	    - Did they over-engineer or add unnecessary features?
49	    - Did they add "nice to haves" that weren't in spec?
50	
51	    **Misunderstandings:**
52	    - Did they interpret requirements differently than intended?
53	    - Did they solve the wrong problem?
54	    - Did they implement the right feature but wrong way?
55	
56	    **Verify by reading code, not by trusting report.**
57	
58	    Report:
59	    - ✅ Spec compliant (if everything matches after code inspection)
60	    - ❌ Issues found: [list specifically what's missing or extra, with file:line references]
61	```
62	
```

## 現状

- **実装サブエージェント**: 1タスクずつ直列。skill が明示的に「複数の implementer を並列 dispatch するな（同じブランチでコミット競合）」と禁止
- **レビュー**: spec → code quality の2段階を直列

今の状態: A1+A2+A3 の実装 DONE → spec review 合格 → **これから code quality review** という位置です。

## 並列化の余地

厳密な並列禁止は「同時に2つの implementer が走ること」だけ。以下は安全に並列可能です:

- **タスク N の review 中に、独立タスク N+1 の implementer を start** — 例: A1-A3 の code quality review を回しつつ、B1 (refresh token script、既存ファイルに触れない独立タスク) を実装
- **同一タスクの spec review と code quality review を同時発火** — skill は直列を推奨だが、守るべきは「quality review 前に spec review が通っていること」。結果を同時に受け取って spec が ❌ なら quality 結果は捨てる、でも OK

依存があるタスク（D2→D3→D4 が同じ `spotify.py` を段階的に改修、など）は直列のまま。

## 提案

| 戦略 | 速度 | リスク |
|---|---|---|
| **A** 今のまま厳密直列 | 遅い | なし |
| **B** 次タスク start を current review と並列 | 2倍速 | review で ❌ が出ると後続が無駄走行するケース稀にあり |
| **C** spec + quality review を同時発火 | 微増 | spec ❌ の quality 結果は捨てるだけ、実害なし |

**B + C の組み合わせが実用的**です（A1-A3 は既に spec 通過なので、今から quality review と B1 implementer を並列 dispatch）。

**どの戦略にしますか？**（指示なければ A のまま直列で code quality review を進めます）
